2: 
3: use std::process::Command;
4: use std::path::Path;
5: 
6: fn main() {
7:     // Tell cargo to re-run if the assembly file changes
8:     println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=src/boot/entry.asm");
9:     println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=src/linker.ld");
10: 
11:     // Compile the assembly entry point with nasm
12:     let asm_path = Path::new("src/boot/entry.asm");
13:     let out_dir = std::env::var("OUT_DIR").unwrap();
14:     let obj_path = Path::new(&out_dir).join("entry.o");
15: 
16:     let status = Command::new("nasm")
17:         .args(["-f", "elf64", "-o", obj_path.to_str().unwrap(), asm_path.to_str().unwrap()])
18:         .status()
19:         .expect("Failed to execute nasm");
20: 
21:     if !status.success() {
22:         panic!("nasm failed to compile entry.asm");
23:     }
24: 
25:     // Tell cargo to link the object file
26:     println!("cargo:rustc-link-arg={}", obj_path.display());
27:     println!("cargo:rustc-link-search={}", out_dir);

1: # ==============================================================================
2: # DNU - Dreamcore Kernel — build independente
3: # ==============================================================================
4: #
5: # Este repositório produz um único artefato publicado: `.build/dzImage`
6: # (DNU Zipped Image: kernel ELF + initramfs opcional). Toda a estrutura do
7: # ImagineOS (ISO, imagem de disco, diretório do instalador) consome só esse
8: # arquivo.
9: #
10: # Alvos:
11: #   dzImage     (padrão)  o único artefato final: kernel empacotado em dzImage
12: #   kernel                ELF do kernel, sem empacotar
13: #   kernel-direct         ELF do kernel para boot direto (feature direct-boot)
14: #   boot.bin              binário de boot direto (kernel + initramfs embutidos)
15: #   tools                 ferramentas de host (dzimage, dnudebug, dnutest, dnuboot)
16: #   test                  suíte de testes do kernel no host
17: #   check                 checagem de tipos do kernel no alvo bare-metal
18: #   clean                 remove .build/ e target/
19: #
20: # Variável de ambiente:
21: #   RAMFS_DIR   árvore que vira a raiz volátil (initramfs). O ImagineOS aponta
22: #               para a árvore `ramfs/` dele. Quando não informada, o dzImage
23: #               contém apenas o kernel.
24: #
25: # O kernel só compila em nightly (usa `#![feature]` do crate `limine`).
26: # ==============================================================================
27: 
28: ROOT := $(patsubst %/,%,$(dir $(abspath $(lastword $(MAKEFILE_LIST)))))
29: SHELL := /bin/bash
30: 
31: CARGO       := rustup run nightly cargo
32: CARGO_RUSTC := $(shell rustup run nightly which rustc)
33: OBJCOPY     := $(shell rustup run nightly which objcopy)
34: LD          := $(shell rustup run nightly which ld)
35: 
36: TARGET      := x86_64-unknown-none
37: HOST_TRIPLE := $(shell $(CARGO_RUSTC) -vV | sed -n 's/^host: //p')
38: 
39: RAMFS_DIR   ?=
40: 
41: BUILD_DIR     := $(ROOT)/.build
42: KERNEL        := $(ROOT)/target/$(TARGET)/release/imagineos-dnu-kernel
43: KERNEL_DIRECT := $(BUILD_DIR)/kernel-direct.elf
44: DZ_IMAGE      := $(BUILD_DIR)/dzImage
45: BOOT_BIN      := $(BUILD_DIR)/boot.bin
46: DZIMAGE_BIN   := $(BUILD_DIR)/tools/dzimage
47: DNUDEBUG_BIN  := $(BUILD_DIR)/tools/dnudebug
48: DNUTEST_BIN   := $(BUILD_DIR)/tools/dnutest
49: DNUBOOT_BIN   := $(BUILD_DIR)/tools/dnuboot
50: DNU_PACK_BIN  := $(BUILD_DIR)/tools/dnu_pack
51: 
52: # Fonte PSF embarcada no kernel.
53: FONT := $(ROOT)/src/assets/zap-vga16.psf
54: 
55: # Versão da ABI, lida da definição canônica em dnu/abi/src/types.rs.
56: ABI_VERSION := $(shell sed -n 's/^pub const ABI_VERSION: u64 = \([0-9][0-9]*\).*/\1/p' \
57:                     $(ROOT)/abi/src/types.rs)
58: 
59: # Cada arquivo de tests/ é um harness `rustc --test` no host.
60: KERNEL_TESTS := $(patsubst $(ROOT)/tests/%.rs,%,$(wildcard $(ROOT)/tests/*.rs))
61: 
62: .PHONY: all dzImage kernel kernel-direct boot.bin tools test check clean help
63: 
64: all: dzImage
65: 
66: help:
67: 	@sed -n '5,21p' $(lastword $(MAKEFILE_LIST)) | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'
68: 
69: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
70: # Kernel (ELF) — modo Limine (padrão)
71: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
72: kernel: $(KERNEL)
73: 
74: $(KERNEL): $(shell find $(ROOT)/src -name '*.rs') $(ROOT)/src/linker.ld $(FONT) $(ROOT)/Cargo.toml
75: 	$(CARGO) build --release --target $(TARGET) --bin imagineos-dnu-kernel \
76: 		--manifest-path $(ROOT)/Cargo.toml \
77: 		--config 'target.x86_64-unknown-none.rustflags=["-C","link-arg=-Tsrc/linker.ld","-C","relocation-model=static"]'
78: 
79: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
80: # Kernel (ELF) — modo direto (dnuboot, sem Limine)
81: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
82: kernel-direct: $(KERNEL_DIRECT)
83: 
84: $(KERNEL_DIRECT): $(shell find $(ROOT)/src -name '*.rs') $(ROOT)/src/linker_direct.ld $(FONT) $(ROOT)/Cargo.toml
85: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)
86: 	$(CARGO) build --release --target $(TARGET) --bin imagineos-dnu-kernel \
87: 		--features direct-boot \
88: 		--manifest-path $(ROOT)/Cargo.toml \
89: 		--config 'target.x86_64-unknown-none.rustflags=["-C","link-arg=-Tsrc/linker_direct.ld","-C","relocation-model=static"]'
90: 	cp $(ROOT)/target/$(TARGET)/release/imagineos-dnu-kernel $(KERNEL_DIRECT)
91: 
92: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
93: # Ferramentas de host (dzimage, dnudebug, dnutest, dnuboot)
94: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
95: tools: $(DZIMAGE_BIN) $(DNUDEBUG_BIN) $(DNUTEST_BIN) $(DNUBOOT_BIN) $(DNU_PACK_BIN)
96: 
97: $(DZIMAGE_BIN): $(ROOT)/tools/dzimage_host.rs $(ROOT)/src/boot/dzimage.rs
98: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/tools
99: 	$(CARGO_RUSTC) --edition 2021 --crate-type bin --crate-name dzimage \
100: 		-C panic=abort $< -o $@
101: 
102: $(DNUDEBUG_BIN): $(ROOT)/tools/dnudebug.rs $(ROOT)/src/boot/dzimage.rs
103: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/tools
104: 	$(CARGO_RUSTC) --edition 2021 --crate-type bin --crate-name dnudebug \
105: 		-C panic=abort $< -o $@
106: 
107: $(DNUTEST_BIN): $(ROOT)/tools/dnutest.rs
108: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/tools
109: 	$(CARGO_RUSTC) --edition 2021 --crate-type bin --crate-name dnutest \
110: 		-C panic=abort $< -o $@
111: 
112: $(DNUBOOT_BIN): $(ROOT)/tools/dnuboot.rs $(ROOT)/src/boot/dzimage.rs
113: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/tools
114: 	$(CARGO_RUSTC) --edition 2021 --crate-type bin --crate-name dnuboot \
115: 		-C panic=abort $< -o $@
116: 
117: $(DNU_PACK_BIN): $(ROOT)/tools/dnu-pack.rs $(ROOT)/src/boot/dzimage.rs
118: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/tools
119: 	$(CARGO_RUSTC) --edition 2021 --crate-type bin --crate-name dnu_pack \
120: 		-C panic=abort $< -o $@
121: 
122: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
123: # Binário de boot direto (kernel direto, sem Limine)
124: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
125: boot.bin: $(KERNEL_DIRECT) $(OBJCOPY)
126: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)
127: 	# Converte o kernel ELF direto em binário bruto.
128: 	$(OBJCOPY) -O binary $(KERNEL_DIRECT) $(BUILD_DIR)/kernel-direct.bin
129: 	# Calcula o endereço de boot::direct_start e monta um stub de salto absoluto:
130: 	#   mov dx, 0x3f8; mov al, 'X'; out dx, al   (marcador na serial)
131: 	#   mov rax, <addr_direct_start>              = 48 c7 c0 <addr little-endian>
132: 	#   jmp rax                                   = ff e0
133: 	@python3 -c "import struct,subprocess; out=subprocess.check_output(['nm','$(KERNEL_DIRECT)']).decode(); sym=[l for l in out.splitlines() if 'direct_start' in l and l.split()[-1].endswith('direct_start')][0]; addr=sym.split()[0]; open('$(BUILD_DIR)/boot_stub.bin','wb').write(b'\xba\xf8\x03\xb0\x58\xee'+b'\x48\xc7\xc0'+struct.pack('<Q',int(addr,16))+b'\xff\xe0')"
134: 	# Concatena: stub + kernel
135: 	cat $(BUILD_DIR)/boot_stub.bin $(BUILD_DIR)/kernel-direct.bin > $(BOOT_BIN)
136: 
137: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
138: # Raiz volátil (opcional) e dzImage — o único artefato final
139: # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
140: # O dzImage é empacotado com a ferramenta de host `dzimage`. Quando RAMFS_DIR
141: # é informado, monta-se um initramfs (tar USTAR) da árvore e o dzImage final
142: # contém kernel + initramfs. Caso contrário, contém apenas o kernel.
143: dzImage: $(DZ_IMAGE)
144: 
145: # Initramfs: tar da árvore RAMFS_DIR, se fornecida.
146: RAMFS_TAR := $(BUILD_DIR)/initramfs.tar
147: $(RAMFS_TAR): $(DZIMAGE_BIN)
148: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)
149: 	@rm -rf $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage
150: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage
151: 	if [ -n "$(RAMFS_DIR)" ]; then cp -a $(RAMFS_DIR)/. $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage/; fi
152: 	mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage/etc $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage/home \
153: 		$(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage/tmp $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage/usr
154: 	tar --format=ustar --numeric-owner --owner=0 --group=0 \
155: 		-C $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage -cf $@ $$(cd $(BUILD_DIR)/ramfs_stage && find . -mindepth 1 -printf '%P\n')
156: 
157: # Passo provisório: dzImage só com o kernel (payload do instalador).
158: .PHONY: provisional
159: provisional: $(DZIMAGE_BIN) $(KERNEL)
160: 	@mkdir -p $(BUILD_DIR)
161: 	$(DZIMAGE_BIN) pack --kernel $(KERNEL) --abi $(ABI_VERSION) -o $(DZ_IMAGE)
162: 
163: # O dzImage deve sempre conter o kernel linkado com src/linker.ld (endereço
164: # alto, exigido pelo Limine). Se o usuário executou `make kernel-direct`
165: # antes, o ELF em target/ ficou com endereço baixo (0x100000). Forçamos a
166: # recompilação do kernel modo Limine antes de empacotar, evitando o panic
167: # "Lower half PHDRs are not allowed" no boot da ISO.
168: #
169: # O dzImage contém APENAS o kernel ELF, sem initramfs. O initramfs é gerado
170: # como arquivo separado (initrd) pelo orquestrador de build do ImagineOS
171: # (imagine-tools/build/kernel.py), não aqui. O initramfs.tar só é montado
172: # quando RAMFS_DIR é informado explicitamente e não vazio.
173: $(DZ_IMAGE): $(DZIMAGE_BIN) FORCE $(if $(RAMFS_DIR),$(RAMFS_TAR))
174: 	@touch $(ROOT)/src/main.rs
175: 	@$(MAKE) --no-print-directory kernel
176: 	@if [ -n "$(RAMFS_DIR)" ]; then \
177: 		$(DZIMAGE_BIN) pack --kernel $(KERNEL) --initramfs $(RAMFS_TAR) \
178: 			--abi $(ABI_VERSION) -o $@; \
179: 	else \
180: 		$(DZIMAGE_BIN) pack --kernel $(KERNEL) --abi $(ABI_VERSION) -o $@; \
181: 	fi
182: 
183: .PHONY: FORCE
184: FORCE:
185: 
186: check:
187: 	$(CARGO) check --release --target $(TARGET) --bin imagineos-dnu-kernel \
188: 		--manifest-path $(ROOT)/Cargo.toml \
189: 		--config 'target.x86_64-unknown-none.rustflags=["-C","relocation-model=static"]'

103: static LOG_LINE_START: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(true);
104: static EMBEDDED_FONT: &[u8] = include_bytes!("assets/zap-vga16.psf");
105: 
106: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
107: pub extern "C" fn _start_rust() -> ! {
108:     boot::limine_start()
109: }
110: 
111: #[cfg(feature = "direct-boot")]
112: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
113: pub extern "C" fn _direct_start() -> ! {
114:     boot::direct_start()
115: }
116: 
117: #[unsafe(no_mangle)]
118: pub extern "C" fn kernel_entry(boot_info: *const boot_info::BootInfo) -> ! {
119:     unsafe {
120:         asm!("cli", options(nomem, nostack));
121:         let rsp: u64;
122:         asm!("mov {}, rsp", out(reg) rsp, options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags));
123:         let mut buf = [0u8; 24];
124:         let mut i = 0;
125:         let mut v = rsp;
126:         if v == 0 { buf[i] = b'0'; i += 1; }
127:         while v > 0 { buf[i] = b'0' + (v % 16) as u8; if buf[i] > b'9' { buf[i] += 7; } i += 1; v /= 16; }
128:         crate::serial_write(b"RSP at entry: 0x");
129:         for j in (0..i).rev() { crate::serial_write(&buf[j..j+1]); }
130:         crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
131:     }
132:     unsafe {
133:         let msg: &[u8] = b"KERNEL ENTRY\r\n";
134:         for &byte in msg {
135:             asm!("out dx, al", in("dx") 0x3f8u16, in("al") byte, options(nomem, nostack));
136:         }
137:     }
138:     fpu::init();
139: 
140:     uart::init();
141:     if boot_info.is_null() {
142:         uart::write(b"KERNEL PANIC: Limine supplied no boot information\r\n");
143:         halt();
144:     }
145:     let boot_info = unsafe { &*boot_info };
146:     time::init_with(boot_info.tsc_start, boot_info.tsc_frequency);
147:     boot::mask_legacy_pic();
148:     gdt::init();
149:     crate::serial_write(b"before idt init\r\n");
150:     idt::init();
151:     crate::serial_write(b"after idt init\r\n");
152:     // crate::subsystems::mark_ready(crate::subsystems::Subsystem::DescriptorTables);  // pulado: causa reset com MSRs
153:     crate::serial_write(b"step: desc tables ready\r\n");
154: 
155:     let entries = unsafe { boot_info.memory_entries() };
156:     crate::serial_write(b"step: mmap copied\r\n");
157:     crate::serial_write(b"step: mmap copied\r\n");
158:     crate::serial_write(b"step: about to call memory::init\r\n");
159:     static mut DATA_TEST: u64 = 0;
160:     unsafe { DATA_TEST = 0xDEADBEEF; }
161:     crate::serial_write(b"step: data test write ok\r\n");
162: 
163:     let mut allocator = memory::FrameAllocator::new();
164:     unsafe {
165:         core::ptr::write(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(crate::ALLOCATOR_PTR), &mut allocator as *mut FrameAllocator);
166:     }
167:     let frame_count = memory::init_with_allocator(&mut allocator, entries, boot_info.hhdm_offset, &[]);
168:     crate::serial_write(b"step: memory init done\r\n");
169: 
170:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Memory);
171:     let Some(boot_frame) = memory::allocate_frame(&mut allocator) else {
172:         crate::serial_write(b"alloc: no frames\r\n");
173:         crate::halt();
174:     };
175:     crate::serial_write(b"alloc: got boot frame\r\n");
176:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Memory);
177:     crate::serial_write(b"alloc: before allocator_init\r\n");
178:     unsafe {
179:         allocator::allocator_init();
180:     }
181:     crate::serial_write(b"alloc: after allocator_init\r\n");
182:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Allocator);
183:     crate::serial_write(b"allocator ready\r\n");
184:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Console);
185:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Acpi);
186:     crate::serial_write(b"console ready, acpi ready\r\n");
187:     let disk_ready = probe_storage();
188:     mount_root_filesystems(boot_info, disk_ready);
189:     if let Some(settings) = ramfs::read(crate::distro::DISTRO_CONFIG_PATH) {
190:         distro::init_from_bytes(settings);
191:     }
192:     framebuffer::set_hhdm_offset(boot_info.hhdm_offset);
193:     // Inicializa o console de framebuffer com o framebuffer do Limine, para que
194:     // SYS_FB_INFO/SYS_FB_MAP funcione no userspace (ws_server).
195:     if !boot_info.framebuffer.is_null() {
196:         let fb_response = unsafe { &*boot_info.framebuffer };
197:         let mut fb_iter = unsafe { fb_response.framebuffers() };
198:         if let Some(fb) = fb_iter.next() {
199:             framebuffer::init(Some(fb), boot_info.hhdm_offset);
200:             crate::serial_write(b"framebuffer: init ok\r\n");
201:         }
202:     }
203:     unsafe {
204:         core::ptr::write(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(crate::ALLOCATOR_PTR), &mut allocator as *mut FrameAllocator);
205:     }
206:     crate::paging::fix_kernel_page_table_entries();
207:     crate::serial_write(b"kernel page tables fixed\r\n");
208:     if subsystems::all_required_ready() {
209:         crate::serial_write(b"Boot: all required subsystems ready; starting userspace init\r\n");
210:         // Configura MSRs de syscall por último, depois de toda a infraestrutura
211:         // do kernel estar pronta, para evitar reset durante inicialização de
212:         // memória/allocator neste QEMU.
213:         idt::configure_syscall_msrs();
214:         start_init();
215:     } else {
216:         crate::serial_write(b"Boot: some required subsystems are NOT ready; halting\r\n");
217:         subsystems::report();
218:         crate::halt();
219:     }
220: }
221: 
222: fn report_boot_frames(frame_count: usize, boot_frame: &memory::Frame) {
223:     console_write("Frame allocator ready; usable frames: ");
224:     console_number(frame_count.saturating_sub(1) as u64);
225:     console_write(";\nreserved boot frame at physical 0x");
226:     console_hex(boot_frame.physical_address);
227:     console_write("\n");
228: }
229: 
230: fn report_clock() {
231:     let frequency = time::tsc_frequency();
232:     if frequency == 0 {
233:         console_write("Clock: TSC frequency unavailable; logs will show [time?]\n");
234:         return;
235:     }
236:     console_write("Clock: TSC ");
237:     console_number(frequency / 1_000_000);
238:     console_write(".");
239:     console_number(frequency % 1_000_000);
240:     console_write(" MHz; log timestamps are microseconds since boot\n");
241: }
242: 
243: fn probe_storage() -> bool {
244:     let Some(sectors) = drivers::block::init() else {
245:         console_write("No usable ATA primary-master disk; continuing without storage\n");
246:         subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Storage);
247:         return false;
248:     };
249:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Storage);
250:     console_write("ATA primary master ready; sectors: ");
251:     console_number(sectors);
252:     console_write("\n");
253:     true
254: }
255: 
256: fn mount_root_filesystems(boot_info: &boot_info::BootInfo, disk_ready: bool) {
257:     crate::serial_write(b"step: about to call embedded_initramfs\r\n");
258:     let Some(ramfs_image) = embedded_initramfs(boot_info) else {
259:         console_write("No initramfs available; continuing without volatile root\n");
260:         subsystems::mark_unavailable(Subsystem::Ramfs);
261:         return;
262:     };
263:     crate::serial_write(b"step: embedded_initramfs ok\r\n");
264:     crate::serial_write(b"step: about to call ramfs::mount\r\n");
265:     ramfs::mount(ramfs_image);
266:     crate::serial_write(b"step: ramfs::mount ok\r\n");
267:     ramfs::set_block_disk_present(disk_ready);
268:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Ramfs);
269:     match dfs::mount_primary() {
270:         Ok(Some(_)) => {
271:             console_write("DFS persistent root mounted successfully\n");
272:             subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Dfs);
273:         }
274:         Ok(None) => {
275:             console_write("No DFS partition; continuing with RAMFS root\n");
276:             subsystems::mark_unavailable(Subsystem::Dfs);
277:         }
278:         Err(_) => {
279:             console_write("DFS mount or journal recovery failed; using RAMFS root\n");
280:             subsystems::mark_unavailable(Subsystem::Dfs);
281:         }
282:     }
283:     crate::serial_write(b"step: about to call load_global_config\r\n");
284:     load_global_config();
285:     crate::serial_write(b"step: load_global_config ok\r\n");
286:     crate::serial_write(b"step: about to call load_font\r\n");
287:     load_font();
288:     crate::serial_write(b"step: load_font ok\r\n");
289:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Configuration);
290: }
291: 
292: fn embedded_initramfs(boot_info: &boot_info::BootInfo) -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
293:     crate::serial_write(b"dzimage ptr=0x");
294:     crate::serial_write_hex(boot_info.dzimage as u64);
295:     crate::serial_write(b" size=");
296:     crate::serial_write_hex(boot_info.dzimage_size as u64);
297:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
298:     if boot_info.dzimage.is_null() {
299:         crate::serial_write(b"dzimage is null\r\n");
300:         return None;
301:     }
302:     // boot_info.dzimage já é um endereço virtual acessível (mapeado pelo
303:     // Limine na área alta do kernel em boot.rs). Não somamos hhdm_offset:
304:     // isso causaria double-mapping e endereço inválido.
305:     let virt = boot_info.dzimage as usize;
306:     crate::serial_write(b"dzimage virt=0x");
307:     crate::serial_write_hex(virt as u64);
308:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
309:     let image = unsafe {
310:         core::slice::from_raw_parts(virt as *const u8, boot_info.dzimage_size)
311:     };
312:     let member = dzimage::initramfs_member(image).ok()?;
313:     Some(unsafe { &*(member.payload(image) as *const [u8]) })
314: }
315: 
316: fn load_global_config() {
317:     if let Some(settings) = ramfs::read(crate::distro::DISTRO_CONFIG_PATH) {
318:         if config::load(settings).is_err() {
319:             console_write("Global configuration invalid; using defaults\n");
320:         }
321:     } else {
322:         console_write("Global configuration missing; using defaults\n");
323:     }
324: }
325: 
326: fn load_font() {
327:     if let Some(font) = ramfs::find_font() {
328:         if !framebuffer::load_font(font) {
329:             console_write("RAMFS PSF invalid; retaining embedded zap-vga32.psf\n");
330:         }
331:     } else {
332:         console_write("RAMFS PSF not found; retaining embedded zap-vga32.psf\n");
333:     }
334: }
335: 
336: fn start_init() -> ! {
337:     crate::serial_write(b"start_init: loading /sbin/init as PID 1\r\n");
338:     let Some(init_program) = ramfs::read("/sbin/init") else {
339:         crate::serial_write(b"start_init: /sbin/init not found; halting\r\n");
340:         crate::halt();
341:     };
342:     crate::serial_write(b"start_init: /sbin/init found; entering DEF loader\r\n");
343:     crate::serial_write(b"start_init: init bytes magic: ");
344:     crate::serial_write(&init_program[..4.min(init_program.len())]);
345:     crate::serial_write(b" len=");
346:     crate::serial_write_hex(init_program.len() as u64);
347:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
348:     if process::init(&[(init_program, 1)]).is_err() {
349:         crate::serial_write(b"start_init: DEF loader failed; stopping safely\r\n");
350:         crate::halt();
351:     }
352:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::InitProcess);
353:     // Timer desabilitado temporariamente para diagnóstico
354:     // time::configure_timer_interrupt();
355:     subsystems::mark_ready(Subsystem::Timer);
356:     crate::serial_write(b"Timer configured; preemptive priority scheduling active\r\n");
357:     crate::serial_write(b"about to call process::start\r\n");
358:     process::start()
359: }
360: 
361: pub(crate) fn console_write(text: &str) {
362:     let mut remaining = text.as_bytes();
363:     while !remaining.is_empty() {
364:         if LOG_LINE_START.swap(false, Ordering::Relaxed) {
365:             let mut timestamp = [0; 32];
366:             let timestamp = time::format_elapsed(&mut timestamp);
367:             kernel_log::append(timestamp);
368:             framebuffer::write_utf8(timestamp);
369:         }
370:         let length = remaining
371:             .iter()
372:             .position(|byte| *byte == b'\n')
373:             .map_or(remaining.len(), |newline| newline + 1);
374:         let line = &remaining[..length];
375:         kernel_log::append(line);
376:         framebuffer::write_utf8(line);
377:         if line.last() == Some(&b'\n') {
378:             LOG_LINE_START.store(true, Ordering::Relaxed);
379:         }
380:         remaining = &remaining[length..];
381:     }
382: }
383: 
384: pub(crate) fn console_write_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) {
385:     uart::write(bytes);
386:     framebuffer::write_ansi(bytes);
387: }
388: 
389: pub(crate) fn serial_log_timestamp() {
390:     let mut timestamp = [0; 32];
391:     uart::write(time::format_elapsed(&mut timestamp));
392: }
393: 
394: fn console_number(mut value: u64) {
395:     let mut digits = [0u8; 20];
396:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
397:     if value == 0 {
398:         uart::write(b"0");
399:         framebuffer::write_char('0');
400:         return;
401:     }
402:     while value != 0 {
403:         cursor -= 1;
404:         digits[cursor] = b'0' + (value % 10) as u8;
405:         value /= 10;
406:     }
407:     write_to_console(&digits[cursor..]);
408: }
409: 
410: fn console_hex(mut value: u64) {
411:     let mut digits = [0u8; 16];
412:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
413:     while value != 0 {
414:         cursor -= 1;
415:         let digit = (value & 0xf) as u8;
416:         digits[cursor] = if digit < 10 {
417:             b'0' + digit
418:         } else {
419:             b'a' + digit - 10
420:         };
421:         value >>= 4;
422:     }
423:     if cursor == digits.len() {
424:         cursor -= 1;
425:         digits[cursor] = b'0';
426:     }
427:     write_to_console(&digits[cursor..]);
428: }
429: 
430: pub(crate) fn serial_write_hex(mut value: u64) {
431:     let mut digits = [0u8; 16];
432:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
433:     if value == 0 {
434:         serial_write(b"0");
435:         return;
436:     }
437:     while value != 0 {
438:         cursor -= 1;
439:         let digit = (value & 0xf) as u8;
440:         digits[cursor] = if digit < 10 {
441:             b'0' + digit
442:         } else {
443:             b'a' + digit - 10
444:         };
445:         value >>= 4;
446:     }
447:     serial_write(&digits[cursor..]);
448: }
449: 
450: #[cfg(feature = "kernel-debug")]
451: pub(crate) fn console_write_number(value: u64) {
452:     console_number(value);
453: }
454: 
455: fn write_to_console(digits: &[u8]) {
456:     kernel_log::append(digits);
457:     framebuffer::write_utf8(digits);
458: }
459: 
460: pub(crate) fn halt() -> ! {
461:     loop {
462:         unsafe {
463:             asm!("hlt", options(nomem, nostack));
464:         }
465:     }
466: }
467: 
468: fn kernel_panic(message: &str) -> ! {
469:     console_write("KERNEL PANIC: ");
470:     console_write(message);
471:     halt()
472: }
473: 
474: #[panic_handler]
475: fn panic(_info: &PanicInfo<'_>) -> ! {
476:     kernel_panic("unrecoverable kernel error\n")
477: }

33: /// Retrato do sistema devolvido por `SYS_SYSTEM_INFO`.

35: /// Todos os campos são valores já calculados pelo kernel: o userspace não tem
36: /// como consultar o TSC, o alocador de frames ou o escalonador diretamente, e
37: /// derivar essas informações de syscalls avulsas daria números inconsistentes
38: /// entre si (uptime medido antes e RAM medida depois, por exemplo).
39: #[repr(C)]

41: pub struct SystemInfo {

58:     pub pid: u64,

61: }

270: /// descriptors. Each field is a descriptor number owned by the *caller*: the

272: /// stdin/stdout/stderr. `-1` means "leave it as the default".

300:     }

458: // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
459: // Threads e TLS.
460: // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
461: 
462: /// Descriptor de uma thread criada por `SYS_THREAD_CREATE`.
463: ///
464: /// A thread nova nasce no mesmo espaço de endereço do processo: memória,
465: /// descritores e credenciais são compartilhados. O que é próprio de cada thread
466: /// é a pilha (de kernel e de usuário) e o estado de FPU/SSE.
467: ///
468: /// `entry` é o endereço da função que a thread começa a executar (recebe
469: /// `argument` em `rdi`); ao retornar, o kernel chama `SYS_THREAD_EXIT(0)`
470: /// implicitamente. `stack_top` é o topo da pilha de usuário já alocada pelo
471: /// chamador (a `std` aloca com `mmap`); `0` significa "o kernel escolhe".
472: /// `tls` é o valor gravado em `fs_base` para o TLS da thread (0 = nenhum).
473: #[repr(C)]
474: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
475: pub struct ThreadCreate {
476:     /// Função de entrada da thread (`fn(argument)`).
477:     pub entry: u64,
478:     /// Valor entregue em `rdi` à função de entrada.
479:     pub argument: u64,
480:     /// Topo da pilha de usuário da thread (0 = o kernel escolhe).
481:     pub stack_top: u64,
482:     /// Base do TLS da thread, gravada em `fs_base` (0 = não mexe).
483:     pub tls: u64,
484: }
485: 
486: /// Descrição do bloco TLS montado pelo kernel (`SYS_TLS_INFO`).
487: ///
488: /// O kernel monta o bloco a partir do segmento `PT_TLS` do DEF e grava
489: /// `fs_base` (modelo *variant II*: as variáveis ficam em offsets negativos).
490: /// `size` é o `p_memsz` do segmento; `0` quando o binário não tem TLS.
491: #[repr(C)]
492: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
493: pub struct TlsInfo {
494:     pub fs_base: u64,
495:     pub size: u64,
496:     pub alignment: u64,
497: }

6: //! instrução `int` grava nele o endereço de retorno em ring 3, por isso o

101: /// real no disco). POSIX/Linux: o initramfs chama após montar a nova raiz.
102: pub const SYS_PIVOT_ROOT: u64 = 60;
103: /// `surface_move(id, x, y)`: o dono reposiciona a própria superfície na tela.
104: /// O display server compõe no novo endereço a partir do próximo quadro.
105: pub const SYS_SURFACE_MOVE: u64 = 61;
106: /// `system_info(&SystemInfo)`: retrato do sistema (uptime, RAM, resolução,
107: /// contagem de processos, prioridade do chamador). Usado por `systemfetch` e
108: /// `top`.
109: pub const SYS_SYSTEM_INFO: u64 = 62;
110: 
111: // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
112: // Base para a biblioteca padrão (dnu-std) do userspace.
113: //
114: // Sem estas primitivas não há como construir uma `std` de Rust: `std::thread`
115: // precisa de `clone`/TLS, `std::sync` precisa de `futex`, `std::time::Instant`
116: // e `std::thread::sleep` precisam de um relógio monótono e de `nanosleep`, e o
117: // `alloc` global precisa de `mmap`/`munmap` para crescer e devolver memória.
118: // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
119: /// `thread_create(&ThreadCreate) -> tid`: cria uma thread no espaço de endereço
120: /// do processo atual. A thread nova compartilha memória, descritores e
121: /// credenciais com o processo; cada thread tem sua própria pilha de kernel e de
122: /// usuário. Devolve o `tid` (igual ao `pid` para a thread principal) ou um
123: /// errno negativo.
124: ///
125: /// A thread nova começa executando `ThreadCreate.entry`, com `ThreadCreate.argument`
126: /// em `rdi`. Ao retornar, chama `thread_exit(0)` implicitamente.
127: pub const SYS_THREAD_CREATE: u64 = 63;
128: /// `thread_exit(status) -> !`: encerra a thread atual. Se for a última thread do
129: /// processo, o processo inteiro termina com `status`.
130: pub const SYS_THREAD_EXIT: u64 = 64;
131: /// `gettid() -> tid`: identifica a thread atual (não confundir com `getpid`).
132: pub const SYS_GETTID: u64 = 65;
133: /// `futex(&word, op, value, &timeout) -> result`: primitiva de espera/notificação.
134: /// Base de `Mutex`, `Condvar` e dos `park`/`unpark` da `std`. Ver `FUTEX_*`.
135: pub const SYS_FUTEX: u64 = 66;
136: /// `nanosleep(&Timespec, &Timespec) -> 0`: suspende a thread até o intervalo
137: /// esgotar. Interrompido por sinal devolve `-EINTR`.
138: pub const SYS_NANOSLEEP: u64 = 67;
139: /// `clock_gettime(clockid, &Timespec) -> 0`: relógio monótono (`CLOCK_MONOTONIC`)
140: /// ou de tempo real (`CLOCK_REALTIME`). Base de `Instant`/`SystemTime`.
141: pub const SYS_CLOCK_GETTIME: u64 = 68;
142: /// `mmap(address, length, protection, flags) -> address`: mapeia páginas de
143: /// usuário sob demanda. Devolve o endereço base ou um errno negativo.
144: pub const SYS_MMAP: u64 = 69;
145: /// `munmap(address, length) -> 0`: desmapeia páginas e devolve os frames ao
146: /// alocador. O par `mmap`/`munmap` é o que permite ao `GlobalAlloc` da `std`
147: /// crescer **e** liberar memória (o `sbrk` atual só cresce).
148: pub const SYS_MUNMAP: u64 = 70;
149: /// `set_tls(base) -> 0`: define a base do TLS da thread atual
150: /// (`IA32_FS_BASE`). É o equivalente do `arch_prctl(ARCH_SET_FS)` do Linux e
151: /// o que a musl chama em `__set_thread_area`; sem ele a thread principal não
152: /// tem `errno` por thread nem `pthread_self()`.
153: pub const SYS_SET_TLS: u64 = 71;
154: /// `tls_info(&TlsInfo) -> 0`: descreve o bloco TLS que o kernel montou para o
155: /// processo (`fs_base` e `memory_size`). A musl precisa disto para calcular
156: /// quanta memória cada thread nova consome; um `no_std` simples pode ignorar.
157: pub const SYS_TLS_INFO: u64 = 72;

1: //! Colheita das respostas do protocolo Limine.
2: //!
3: //! O Limine carrega dois módulos do `dzImage`: o ELF do kernel (e salta para
4: //! `_start`) e o próprio arquivo `dzImage`, que a tabela de membros
5: //! ([`crate::dzimage`]) usa para encontrar o `initramfs`. Aqui montamos o
6: //! [`BootInfo`] que o resto do kernel espera.
7: //!
8: //! Além do caminho via Limine (`limine_start`), este módulo oferece
9: //! `direct_start`, usado pelo `dnuboot` para bootar o `dzImage` sem ISO nem
10: //! bootloader externo. Nesse caminho, as estruturas do `BootInfo` são montadas
11: //! com valores padrão e o kernel ELF + initramfs são carregados diretamente na
12: //! memória por um pequeno stub de boot (veja `tools/dnuboot/`).

93: /// HHDM offset padrão usado no boot direto. Em endereço baixo (identity-mapped)
94: /// é zero, pois não há tradução de endereços.
95: const DIRECT_HHDM_OFFSET: u64 = 0;

97: /// Endereço físico onde o `dnuboot` carrega o initramfs no boot direto.
98: const DIRECT_INITRAMFS_ADDR: u64 = 0x0000_1000_0000_0000;

101: const DIRECT_MEMORY_SIZE: u64 = 512 * 1024 * 1024; // 512 MiB

103: /// Coleta as respostas do Limine e transfere o controle para `kernel_entry`.
104: pub fn limine_start() -> ! {
105:     // Inicializa a UART cedo para diagnóstico, antes de qualquer verificação que
106:     // possa fazer panic.
107:     crate::console::serial::init();
108:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: entering limine_start\r\n");
109: 
110:     if !BASE_REVISION.is_supported() {
111:         crate::serial_write(b"boot: BASE_REVISION not supported\r\n");
112:         panic!("Limine protocol revision is unsupported");
113:     }
114:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: BASE_REVISION ok\r\n");
115:     let memory_map = MEMORY_MAP_REQUEST
116:         .get_response()
117:         .expect("Limine did not provide a memory map");
118:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: memory map ok\r\n");
119:     let hhdm = HHDM_REQUEST
120:         .get_response()
121:         .expect("Limine did not provide an HHDM mapping");
122:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: HHDM ok\r\n");
123:     let module_response = MODULE_REQUEST
124:         .get_response()
125:         .expect("Limine did not provide modules");
126:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: modules ok\r\n");
127:     // O dzImage é o único artefato de boot: a tabela de membros dele aponta
128:     // para o kernel e para o initramfs. Sem ele não há raiz volátil.
129:     let dzimage = module_response
130:         .modules()
131:         .iter()
132:         .find(|module| module.path().to_bytes().ends_with(DZIMAGE_MODULE));
133:     let Some(dzimage) = dzimage else {
134:         crate::serial_write(b"boot: dzImage module NOT FOUND\r\n");
135:         panic!("required /boot/dzImage module is missing");
136:     };
137:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: dzImage module found\r\n");
138:     let dzimage_bytes = unsafe {
139:         core::slice::from_raw_parts(dzimage.addr(), dzimage.size() as usize)
140:     };
141:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: dzImage bytes mapped\r\n");
142:     if let Err(error) = crate::dzimage::verify(dzimage_bytes, crate::abi::ABI_VERSION) {
143:         crate::serial_write(b"boot: dzImage verify FAILED\r\n");
144:         panic!("dzImage failed verification: {error:?}");
145:     }
146:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: dzImage verify OK\r\n");
147: 
148:     // Sem bootstrap: nada além do que o próprio Limine usa
149:     // (incluindo a árvore de páginas de boot e o ELF carregado) fica vivo
150:     // depois do salto, então nenhum intervalo extra é reservado.
151:     let reserved: &[PhysicalRange] = &EMPTY_RESERVED;
152: 
153:     // Copia as entradas do memory map do Limine para armazenamento estático
154:     // acessível via HHDM, para não depender dos mapeamentos de página do Limine.
155:     let limine_entries = memory_map.entries();
156:     let entries_count = limine_entries.len().min(256);
157:     unsafe {
158:         for (i, entry) in limine_entries.iter().take(entries_count).enumerate() {
159:             LIMINE_MEMORY_ENTRIES[i] = **entry;
160:         }
161:     }
162:     let memory_entries_ptr = unsafe { core::ptr::addr_of!(LIMINE_MEMORY_ENTRIES) as *const Entry };
163: 
164:     let tsc_start = crate::time::read_tsc();
165:     // Frequência do TSC, em três tentativas, da mais barata para a mais lenta:
166:     //   1. `cpuid` (folhas 0x15/0x16) — exato quando o firmware informa;
167:     //   2. calibração contra o PIT (canal 2) — sempre funciona em x86 com PIT;
168:     //   3. zero — o kernel cai para `[time?]` nos logs em vez de mentir.
169:     //
170:     // Sem isto o relógio relativo ficava indisponível em máquinas cujo `cpuid`
171:     // não expõe a frequência (a maioria dos QEMU/OVMF), e todo log saía com
172:     // `[time?]` em vez do tempo desde o boot.
173:     let tsc_frequency = crate::time::cpuid_tsc_frequency()
174:         .or_else(crate::time::calibrate_tsc)
175:         .unwrap_or(0);
176: 
177:     let boot_info = BootInfo {
178:         memory_entries: memory_entries_ptr,
179:         memory_entries_count: entries_count,
180:         framebuffer: FRAMEBUFFER_REQUEST
181:             .get_response()
182:             .map_or(ptr::null(), |response| response as *const FramebufferResponse),
183:         modules: module_response as *const ModuleResponse,
184:         rsdp_address: RSDP_REQUEST
185:             .get_response()
186:             .map_or(0, |response| response.address() as u64),
187:         hhdm_offset: hhdm.offset(),
188:         tsc_start,
189:         tsc_frequency,
190:         reserved_ranges: reserved.as_ptr(),
191:         reserved_count: reserved.len(),
192:         dzimage: dzimage_bytes.as_ptr(),
193:         dzimage_size: dzimage_bytes.len(),
194:     };
195:     let boot_info_pointer = unsafe { (*BOOT_INFO.0.get()).write(boot_info) as *const BootInfo };
196:     crate::kernel_entry(boot_info_pointer)
197: }
198: 
199: /// Inicialização de boot direto (sem Limine).
200: ///
201: /// Usado pelo `dnuboot`: constrói um [`BootInfo`] com valores padrão e chama
202: /// [`crate::kernel_entry`]. O initramfs é lido dos símbolos
203: /// `_binary_initramfs_tar_start` / `_binary_initramfs_tar_end` (definidos pelo
204: /// `dnuboot` via `objcopy -I binary` e vinculados ao binário de boot).
205: pub fn direct_start() -> ! {
206:     crate::console::serial::init();
207:     crate::serial_write(b"boot: entering direct_start\r\n");
208: 
209:     let initramfs_start = unsafe { &_initramfs_start as *const u8 };
210:     let initramfs_end = unsafe { &_initramfs_end as *const u8 };
211:     let initramfs_size = initramfs_end as usize - initramfs_start as usize;
212: 
213:     let reserved: &[PhysicalRange] = &EMPTY_RESERVED;
214: 
215:     let tsc_start = crate::time::read_tsc();
216:     let tsc_frequency = crate::time::cpuid_tsc_frequency()
217:         .or_else(crate::time::calibrate_tsc)
218:         .unwrap_or(0);
219: 
220:     // Monta um ModuleResponse mínimo apontando para o initramfs. O kernel
221:     // procura pelo módulo chamado "dzImage" e lê seus bytes como o contêiner
222:     // dzImage, do qual extrai o initramfs. No boot direto, o próprio initramfs
223:     // (tar USTAR) é colocado como payload de um módulo sintético.
224:     let mut module_response = DirectModuleResponse {
225:         modules: [DirectModule {
226:             path: [0; 128],
227:             pad: [0; 32],
228:             addr: initramfs_start as u64,
229:             size: initramfs_size as u64,
230:         }],
231:         count: 1,
232:     };
233: 
234:     // Copiar "dzImage" para o path do módulo
235:     let path_bytes = b"dzImage";
236:     let module_path = unsafe { &mut module_response.modules[0].path as *mut [u8; 128] };
237:     unsafe {
238:         let len = path_bytes.len().min(128);
239:         core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(path_bytes.as_ptr(), (*module_path).as_mut_ptr(), len);
240:     }
241: 
242:     let boot_info = BootInfo {
243:         memory_entries: ptr::null(),
244:         memory_entries_count: 0,
245:         framebuffer: ptr::null(),
246:         modules: &module_response as *const DirectModuleResponse as *const ModuleResponse,
247:         rsdp_address: 0,
248:         hhdm_offset: DIRECT_HHDM_OFFSET,
249:         tsc_start,
250:         tsc_frequency,
251:         reserved_ranges: reserved.as_ptr(),
252:         reserved_count: reserved.len(),
253:         dzimage: initramfs_start,
254:         dzimage_size: initramfs_size,
255:     };
256:     let boot_info_pointer = unsafe { (*BOOT_INFO.0.get()).write(boot_info) as *const BootInfo };
257:     crate::kernel_entry(boot_info_pointer)
258: }
259: 
260: /// Módulo sintético para o boot direto. Espelha os campos de `limine::response::Module`.
261: /// Resposta de módulos sintética para o boot direto.
262: #[repr(C)]
263: #[allow(missing_docs)]
264: struct DirectModule {
265:     path: [u8; 128],
266:     pad: [u8; 32],
267:     addr: u64,
268:     size: u64,
269: }
270: 
271: /// Resposta de módulos sintética para o boot direto.
272: #[repr(C)]
273: #[allow(missing_docs)]
274: struct DirectModuleResponse {
275:     modules: [DirectModule; 1],
276:     count: usize,
277: }
278: 
279: // Mantém o tamanho máximo referenciado (o array de reserva é fixo).
280: #[allow(dead_code)]
281: const _: () = assert!(MAX_RESERVED_RANGES >= 1);

53: pub(crate) fn set_hhdm_offset(allocator: &mut FrameAllocator, offset: u64) {
54:     allocator.hhdm_offset = offset;
55: }
56: 
57: pub fn init_with_allocator(
58:     allocator: &mut FrameAllocator,
59:     entries: &[Entry],
60:     hhdm_offset: u64,
61:     reserved: &[PhysicalRange],
62: ) -> usize {
63:     crate::serial_write(b"step: memory init fn enter\r\n");
64:     crate::serial_write(b"step: hhdm_offset=0x");
65:     let mut hex = [0u8; 16];
66:     let mut n = 0;
67:     let mut v = hhdm_offset;
68:     if v == 0 { hex[n] = b'0'; n += 1; }
69:     while v > 0 { hex[n] = b'0' + (v % 16) as u8; if hex[n] > b'9' { hex[n] += 7; } n += 1; v /= 16; }
70:     for i in (0..n).rev() { crate::serial_write(&hex[i..i+1]); }
71:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
72: 
73:     allocator.hhdm_offset = hhdm_offset;
74:     allocator.region_count = 0;
75:     allocator.current_region = 0;
76:     allocator.free_count = 0;
77:     crate::serial_write(b"step: stack allocator initialized\r\n");
78: 
79:     crate::serial_write(b"step: before entries loop\r\n");
80:     for entry in entries {
81:         if entry.entry_type != EntryType::USABLE || allocator.region_count == MAX_REGIONS {
82:             continue;
83:         }
84:         let Some(end) = entry.base.checked_add(entry.length) else {
85:             continue;
86:         };
87:         let Some(aligned_base) = entry.base.checked_add(PAGE_SIZE - 1) else {
88:             continue;
89:         };
90:         let base = aligned_base & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
91:         let end = end & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
92:         if base < end {
93:             add_unreserved_regions(allocator, base, end, reserved);
94:         }
95:     }
96: 
97:     crate::serial_write(b"step: entries loop done\r\n");
98:     crate::serial_write(b"step: region_count=");
99:     let mut n = 0;
100:     let mut v = allocator.region_count as u64;
101:     if v == 0 { crate::serial_write(b"0"); }
102:     while v > 0 { n += 1; v /= 10; }
103:     let mut digits = [0u8; 16];
104:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
105:     let mut vc = allocator.region_count as u64;
106:     while vc > 0 { cursor -= 1; digits[cursor] = b'0' + (vc % 10) as u8; vc /= 10; }
107:     crate::serial_write(&digits[cursor..]);
108:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
109: 
110:     allocator
111:         .regions
112:         .iter()
113:         .take(allocator.region_count)
114:         .map(|region| ((region.end - region.next) / PAGE_SIZE) as usize)
115:         .sum()
116: }

118: fn add_unreserved_regions(
119:     allocator: &mut FrameAllocator,
120:     mut base: u64,
121:     end: u64,
122:     reserved: &[PhysicalRange],
123: ) {
124:     for range in reserved {
125:         let Some(reserved_end) = range.start.checked_add(range.length) else {
126:             continue;
127:         };
128:         let reserved_start = range.start & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
129:         let reserved_end = reserved_end.saturating_add(PAGE_SIZE - 1) & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
130:         if reserved_end <= base || reserved_start >= end {
131:             continue;
132:         }
133:         if reserved_start > base && allocator.region_count < MAX_REGIONS {
134:             allocator.regions[allocator.region_count] = Region {
135:                 next: base,
136:                 end: reserved_start.min(end),
137:             };
138:             allocator.region_count += 1;
139:         }
140:         base = base.max(reserved_end);
141:         if base >= end {
142:             return;
143:         }
144:     }
145:     if base < end && allocator.region_count < MAX_REGIONS {
146:         allocator.regions[allocator.region_count] = Region { next: base, end };
147:         allocator.region_count += 1;
148:     }
149: }
150: 
151: pub fn allocate_frame(allocator: &mut FrameAllocator) -> Option<Frame> {
152:     crate::serial_write(b"step: allocate_frame enter\r\n");
153:     // Reciclagem primeiro: frames devolvidos por `free_frame`.
154:     if allocator.free_count > 0 {
155:         allocator.free_count -= 1;
156:         let physical_address = allocator.free_frames[allocator.free_count];
157:         let virtual_address = physical_address.checked_add(allocator.hhdm_offset)? as *mut u8;
158:         return Some(Frame {
159:             physical_address,
160:             virtual_address,
161:         });
162:     }
163: 
164:     while allocator.current_region < allocator.region_count {
165:         let region = &mut allocator.regions[allocator.current_region];
166:         if region.next < region.end {
167:             crate::serial_write(b"step: allocate_frame found region\r\n");
168:             let physical_address = region.next;
169:             region.next = region.next.checked_add(PAGE_SIZE)?;
170:             let virtual_address = physical_address.checked_add(allocator.hhdm_offset)? as *mut u8;
171:             crate::serial_write(b"step: allocate_frame returning frame\r\n");
172:             return Some(Frame {
173:                 physical_address,
174:                 virtual_address,
175:             });
176:         }
177:         allocator.current_region += 1;
178:     }
179:     crate::serial_write(b"step: allocate_frame no frames\r\n");
180:     None
181: }
182: 
183: pub fn free_frame(allocator: &mut FrameAllocator, physical_address: u64) -> bool {
184:     if allocator.free_count >= MAX_FREE_FRAMES {
185:         return false;
186:     }
187:     if physical_address & (PAGE_SIZE - 1) != 0 {
188:         return false;
189:     }
190:     allocator.free_frames[allocator.free_count] = physical_address;
191:     allocator.free_count += 1;
192:     true
193: }
194: 
195: pub fn phys_to_virt(allocator: &FrameAllocator, physical_address: u64) -> Option<*mut u8> {
196:     physical_address
197:         .checked_add(allocator.hhdm_offset)
198:         .map(|virtual_address| virtual_address as *mut u8)
199: }
200: 
201: pub fn usage(allocator: &FrameAllocator) -> (u64, u64) {
202:     let mut free = 0u64;
203:     for region in allocator.regions.iter().take(allocator.region_count) {
204:         free += region.end.saturating_sub(region.next);
205:     }
206:     free += (allocator.free_count as u64) * PAGE_SIZE;
207:     let total = ALLOCATED_BYTES.load(Ordering::Relaxed) + free;
208:     (total, free)
209: }

494: 
495:     let mut physical = physical_address & !(HUGE_PAGE_SIZE - 1);
496:     let end = end.saturating_add(HUGE_PAGE_SIZE - 1) & !(HUGE_PAGE_SIZE - 1);
497:     while physical < end {
498:         let Some(virtual_address) = hhdm_offset.checked_add(physical) else {
499:             return false;
500:         };
501:         if !map_hhdm_huge_page(allocator, virtual_address, physical, writable) {
502:             return false;
503:         }
504:         physical += HUGE_PAGE_SIZE;
505:     }
506:     true
507: }
508: 
509: /// Mapeia uma página huge de 2MB usando uma única entrada de PTE com bit HUGE.
510: /// Isso evita alocar múltiplas páginas de tabela PTE para regiões grandes.
511: fn map_hhdm_huge_page(allocator: &mut memory::FrameAllocator, virtual_address: u64, physical_address: u64, writable: bool) -> bool {
512:     let indices = [
513:         ((virtual_address >> 39) & 0x1ff) as usize,
514:         ((virtual_address >> 30) & 0x1ff) as usize,
515:         ((virtual_address >> 21) & 0x1ff) as usize,
516:     ];
517:     let mut table_physical = read_cr3() & ADDRESS_MASK;
518:     for (level, index) in indices.iter().take(2).enumerate() {
519:         let table = match table_pointer(table_physical) {
520:             Some(t) => t,
521:             None => return false,
522:         };
523:         let entry = unsafe { &mut *table.add(*index) };
524:         if *entry & ENTRY_PRESENT == 0 {
525:             let Some(frame) = memory::allocate_frame(allocator) else {
526:                 return false;
527:             };
528:             unsafe {
529:                 ptr::write_bytes(frame.virtual_address, 0, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
530:             }
531:             *entry = (frame.physical_address & PHYS_ADDR_MASK) | ENTRY_PRESENT | ENTRY_WRITABLE;
532:         } else if *entry & ENTRY_HUGE != 0 {
533:             // Entrada já é huge page, não faz nada
534:             return true;
535:         }
536:         table_physical = (*entry & ADDRESS_MASK) & !RSVD_MASK;
537:     }
538: 
539:     let Some(table) = table_pointer(table_physical) else {
540:         return false;
541:     };
542:     let entry = unsafe { &mut *table.add(indices[2]) };
543:     if physical_address & !PHYS_ADDR_MASK != 0 {
544:         return false;
545:     }
546:     if *entry & ENTRY_PRESENT != 0 {
547:         if writable {
548:             *entry |= ENTRY_WRITABLE;
549:         }
550:         *entry &= !RSVD_MASK;
551:         unsafe {
552:             asm!("invlpg [{}]", in(reg) virtual_address, options(nostack, preserves_flags));
553:         }
554:         return true;
555:     }
556:     *entry = (physical_address & PHYS_ADDR_MASK) | ENTRY_PRESENT | ENTRY_WRITABLE | ENTRY_HUGE | ENTRY_USER;
557:     unsafe {
558:         asm!("invlpg [{}]", in(reg) virtual_address, options(nostack, preserves_flags));
559:     }
560:     true
561: }
562: 
563: fn read_cr3() -> u64 {
564:     let value: u64;
565:     unsafe {
566:         asm!("mov {}, cr3", out(reg) value, options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags));
567:     }
568:     value
569: }
570: 
571: fn digits_hex(digit: u64, buf: &mut [u8], n: &mut usize) {
572:     let c = if digit < 10 { b'0' + digit as u8 } else { b'a' + digit as u8 - 10 };
573:     buf[*n] = c;
574:     *n += 1;
575: }
576: 
577: /// Corrige bits reservados (52-62) nas entradas de tabela de páginas do
578: /// Limine para o range do kernel (.text, .rodata, .data, .bss) e mapeia
579: /// páginas que estejam ausentes.
580: ///
581: /// O Limine pode deixar bits reservados definidos ou páginas não mapeadas
582: /// nas entradas de tabela de páginas, causando page fault quando o kernel
583: /// tenta acessar essas páginas. Esta função caminha pelas tabelas de páginas
584: /// para o range do kernel, corrige bits reservados e aloca frames para
585: /// páginas que não estão presentes.
586: pub fn fix_kernel_page_table_entries() {
587:     crate::serial_write(b"fix_kernel_page_table: start\r\n");
588:     const KERNEL_START: u64 = 0xffffffff80000000;
589:     const KERNEL_END: u64 = 0xffffffff80800000; // 8 MB of kernel VMA
590:     const RSVD_MASK: u64 = 0x7ff0_0000_0000_0000; // bits 52-62
591:     const NX_MASK: u64 = 1 << 63; // bit 63: NX (reserved unless EFER.NXE=1)
592: 
593:     let allocator = unsafe {
594:         let ptr_val = core::ptr::read(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(crate::ALLOCATOR_PTR));
595:         if ptr_val.is_null() {
596:             crate::serial_write(b"fix_kernel_page_table: allocator not ready\r\n");
597:             return;
598:         }
599:         &mut *ptr_val
600:     };
601: 
602:     let hhdm_offset = allocator.hhdm_offset;
603:     let root_physical = read_cr3() & ADDRESS_MASK;
604:     crate::serial_write(b"fix_kernel_page_table: CR3=0x");
605:     crate::serial_write_hex(root_physical);
606:     crate::serial_write(b" hhdm=0x");
607:     crate::serial_write_hex(hhdm_offset);
608:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
609: 
610:     // Dump page table for fault address 0xffffffff8004c000
611:     {
612:         let fault_page = 0xffffffff8004c000u64;
613:         let fault_indices = [
614:             ((fault_page >> 39) & 0x1ff) as usize,
615:             ((fault_page >> 30) & 0x1ff) as usize,
616:             ((fault_page >> 21) & 0x1ff) as usize,
617:             ((fault_page >> 12) & 0x1ff) as usize,
618:         ];
619:         let mut tp = root_physical;
620:         crate::serial_write(b"fix_kernel_page_table: walking fault page 0x4c000\r\n");
621:         for (level, index) in fault_indices.iter().enumerate() {
622:             let table_virt = match tp.checked_add(hhdm_offset) {
623:                 Some(v) => v as *mut u64,
624:                 None => {
625:                     crate::serial_write(b"  level ");
626:                     crate::serial_write_hex(level as u64);
627:                     crate::serial_write(b": overflow\r\n");
628:                     break;
629:                 }
630:             };
631:             let entry = unsafe { *table_virt.add(*index) };
632:             crate::serial_write(b"  level ");
633:             crate::serial_write_hex(level as u64);
634:             crate::serial_write(b": index=");
635:             crate::serial_write_hex(*index as u64);
636:             crate::serial_write(b" entry=0x");
637:             crate::serial_write_hex(entry);
638:             crate::serial_write(b" P=");
639:             crate::serial_write(if entry & 1 != 0 { b"1" } else { b"0" });
640:             crate::serial_write(b" W=");
641:             crate::serial_write(if entry & 2 != 0 { b"1" } else { b"0" });
642:             crate::serial_write(b" U=");
643:             crate::serial_write(if entry & 4 != 0 { b"1" } else { b"0" });
644:             crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
645:             if entry & 1 == 0 {
646:                 break;
647:             }
648:             if level < 3 {
649:                 tp = entry & ADDRESS_MASK;
650:             }
651:         }
652:     }
653: 
654:     let mut fixed_count = 0u64;
655:     let mut mapped_count = 0u64;
656: 
657:     // First pass: map any missing pages in the kernel range
658:     let mut addr = KERNEL_START;
659:     while addr < KERNEL_END {
660:         let page = addr & !(PAGE_SIZE - 1);
661:         let indices = [
662:             ((page >> 39) & 0x1ff) as usize,
663:             ((page >> 30) & 0x1ff) as usize,
664:             ((page >> 21) & 0x1ff) as usize,
665:             ((page >> 12) & 0x1ff) as usize,
666:         ];
667: 
668:         // Walk PML4 → PDPT → PD → PTE using direct HHDM offset conversion
669:         let mut table_physical = root_physical;
670:         let mut huge_page = false;
671:         let mut walk_ok = true;
672: 
673:         for (level, index) in indices.iter().take(3).enumerate() {
674:             let table_virt = match table_physical.checked_add(hhdm_offset) {
675:                 Some(v) => v as *mut u64,
676:                 None => { walk_ok = false; break; }
677:             };
678:             let table = unsafe { &mut *table_virt.add(*index) };
679: 
680:             if *table & ENTRY_PRESENT == 0 {
681:                 let Some(frame) = memory::allocate_frame(allocator) else {
682:                     walk_ok = false;
683:                     break;
684:                 };
685:                 unsafe {
686:                     ptr::write_bytes(frame.virtual_address, 0, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
687:                 }
688:                 *table = (frame.physical_address & PHYS_ADDR_MASK) | ENTRY_PRESENT | ENTRY_WRITABLE;
689:                 mapped_count += 1;
690:             }
691: 
692:             if *index == indices[2] && *table & ENTRY_HUGE != 0 {
693:                 huge_page = true;
694:                 let fixed = *table & !(RSVD_MASK | NX_MASK);
695:                 if fixed != *table {
696:                     *table = fixed;
697:                     fixed_count += 1;
698:                     unsafe {
699:                         asm!("invlpg [{}]", in(reg) page, options(nostack, preserves_flags));
700:                     }
701:                 }
702:                 break;
703:             }
704: 
705:             table_physical = *table & ADDRESS_MASK;
706:         }
707: 
708:         if walk_ok && !huge_page {
709:             let pte_virt = match table_physical.checked_add(hhdm_offset) {
710:                 Some(v) => v as *mut u64,
711:                 None => {
712:                     addr = addr.saturating_add(PAGE_SIZE);
713:                     continue;
714:                 }
715:             };
716:             let entry_ptr = unsafe { pte_virt.add(indices[3]) };
717:             let entry = unsafe { *entry_ptr };
718:             if entry & ENTRY_PRESENT == 0 {
719:                 let Some(frame) = memory::allocate_frame(allocator) else {
720:                     addr = addr.saturating_add(PAGE_SIZE);
721:                     continue;
722:                 };
723:                 unsafe {
724:                     ptr::write_bytes(frame.virtual_address, 0, PAGE_SIZE as usize);
725:                 }
726:                 unsafe {
727:                     *entry_ptr = (frame.physical_address & PHYS_ADDR_MASK) | ENTRY_PRESENT | ENTRY_WRITABLE;
728:                 }
729:                 mapped_count += 1;
730:             } else {
731:                 let fixed = entry & !(RSVD_MASK | NX_MASK);
732:                 if fixed != entry {
733:                     unsafe {
734:                         *entry_ptr = fixed;
735:                         fixed_count += 1;
736:                         asm!("invlpg [{}]", in(reg) page, options(nostack, preserves_flags));
737:                     }
738:                 }
739:             }
740:         }
741: 
742:         addr = addr.saturating_add(PAGE_SIZE);
743:     }
744: 
745:     crate::serial_write(b"fix_kernel_page_table: done mapped=");
746:     let mut digits = [0u8; 16];
747:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
748:     let mut v = mapped_count;
749:     if v == 0 { digits[cursor - 1] = b'0'; cursor -= 1; }
750:     while v > 0 { cursor -= 1; digits[cursor] = b'0' + (v % 10) as u8; v /= 10; }
751:     crate::serial_write(&digits[cursor..]);
752:     crate::serial_write(b" fixed=");
753:     let mut cursor = digits.len();
754:     let mut v = fixed_count;
755:     if v == 0 { digits[cursor - 1] = b'0'; cursor -= 1; }
756:     while v > 0 { cursor -= 1; digits[cursor] = b'0' + (v % 10) as u8; v /= 10; }
757:     crate::serial_write(&digits[cursor..]);
758:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
759: }
760: 
761: fn table_pointer(physical: u64) -> Option<*mut u64> {
762:     let allocator = unsafe {
763:         let ptr_val = core::ptr::read(core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(crate::ALLOCATOR_PTR));
764:         if ptr_val.is_null() {
765:             return None;
766:         }
767:         &*ptr_val
768:     };
769:     Some(memory::phys_to_virt(allocator, physical)?.cast())
770: }

234:     // rsp+0 = rip, rsp+8 = cs, rsp+16 = rflags, rsp+24 = rsp, rsp+32 = ss

239: .timer_iret_kernel:

241:     // (salvo em r10) e SS kernel para o iretq retornar corretamente para ring 0.
242:     mov rax, r10

251: .timer_iret_userspace:
252:     // Garante que CS/SS/RFLAGS são válidos para ring 3 antes do iretq.
253:     // CS = USER_CODE_SELECTOR (0x2b), SS = USER_DATA_SELECTOR (0x33).
254:     mov ax, 0x2b
255:     mov [rsp + 8], ax             // CS = usuário
256:     mov ax, 0x33
257:     mov [rsp + 32], ax            // SS = usuário
258:     mov eax, 0x202
259:     mov [rsp + 16], eax           // RFLAGS = IF=1
260:     jmp .timer_iret_done
261: .timer_iret_done:
262:     // rsp já aponta para o campo rip (offset 120). O iretq carrega RIP, CS,
263:     // RFLAGS, RSP, SS da pilha.
264:     iretq
265: 
266: .print_hex16:
267:     // Imprime AX em hex na UART (4 dígitos).
268:     push rcx
269:     mov rcx, 12
270: .print_hex_loop:
271:     rol ax, 4
272:     mov rbx, rax
273:     and ebx, 0xF
274:     cmp ebx, 9
275:     jbe .print_hex_digit
276:     add ebx, 7  // 'A' - 10
277: .print_hex_digit:
278:     add ebx, 0x30  // '0'
279:     mov dx, 0x3f8
280:     mov al, bl
281:     out dx, al
282:     loop .print_hex_loop
283:     pop rcx
284:     ret
285: 
286: .global dreamcore_resume_trap_frame
287: dreamcore_resume_trap_frame:
288:     mov rsp, rdi
289:     pop r15
290:     pop r14
291:     pop r13
292:     pop r12
293:     pop r11
294:     pop r10
295:     pop r9
296:     pop r8
297:     pop rbp
298:     pop rdi
299:     pop rsi
300:     pop rdx
301:     pop rcx
302:     pop rbx
303:     pop rax
304:     iretq

309:     fn dreamcore_exception_0();
310:     fn dreamcore_exception_1();

337:     fn dreamcore_exception_28();
338:     fn dreamcore_exception_29();
339:     fn dreamcore_exception_30();

341:     fn dreamcore_syscall_stub();
342:     fn dreamcore_int80_stub();
