1: //! Parser do formato **DEF** (Dreamcore Executable Format) no kernel.
2: //!
3: //! Este módulo dá ao loader de processos a visão de *segmentos carregáveis* do
4: //! DEF: `entry_point`, lista de segmentos, tamanho de pilha e capacidades. O
5: //! DEF é o único formato de userspace aceito pelo kernel; o ELF existe apenas
6: //! como saída intermediária do toolchain, convertida por `dnu-pack`.
7: //!
8: //! A estrutura de bytes do cabeçalho está documentada (e compartilhada com a
9: //! ferramenta `dnu-pack`) em `crate::abi::def`. Aqui ficam apenas as leituras
10: //! defensivas — sem pânico, com aritmética checada — que o kernel precisa para
11: //! tratar um arquivo potencialmente malformado como um `Err` em vez de um
12: //! travamento.
13: //!
14: //! Referência de layout (little-endian; ver o módulo da ABI para o mapa completo):
15: //!
16: //! ```text
17: //!   0   4  magic "DNUF"
18: //!   4   1  arch
19: //!   5   1  binary_type
20: //!   6   2  format_version
21: //!   8   4  abi_version
22: //!   12  4  flags
23: //!   16  4  segment_count
24: //!   20  4  reserved
25: //!   24  8  entry_point
26: //!   32  8  stack_size
27: //!   40  8  image_base
28: //!   48  8  image_size
29: //!   56  32 capabilities
30: //!   88  64 signature
31: //!   152 .. tabela de segmentos (DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE cada)
32: //! ```
33: 
34: use crate::abi::def::{
35:     DEF_FORMAT_VERSION, DEF_HEADER_SIZE, DEF_MAGIC, DEF_MAX_SEGMENTS, DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE, DefArch,
36:     DefPermissions,
37: };
38: 
39: /// Erros de parsing de um DEF.
40: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
41: pub enum Error {
42:     Truncated,
43:     InvalidMagic,
44:     UnsupportedFormat,
45:     UnsupportedArch,
46:     IncompatibleAbi,
47:     InvalidSegments,
48:     InvalidSegment,
49:     InvalidEntry,
50: }
51: 
52: /// Um segmento carregável de um DEF, já validado.
53: #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
54: pub struct Segment {
55:     pub virtual_address: u64,
56:     pub memory_size: u64,
57:     pub file_offset: usize,
58:     pub file_size: usize,
59:     /// Permissões de página (R/W/X). Ver [`DefPermissions`].
60:     pub permissions: u32,
61:     /// Este segmento é o bloco TLS (`.tdata` + `.tbss`).
62:     ///
63:     /// Não é carregado como página comum: `virtual_address` aqui é o endereço
64:     /// **dentro da imagem TLS** (offset 0 = início do bloco), e o loader monta
65:     /// um bloco próprio no topo da pilha, com o TCB logo depois.
66:     pub is_tls: bool,
67: }
68: 
69: impl Segment {
70:     pub fn is_executable(&self) -> bool {
71:         self.permissions & DefPermissions::EXECUTE != 0
72:     }
73: 
74:     pub fn is_writable(&self) -> bool {
75:         self.permissions & DefPermissions::WRITE != 0
76:     }
77: }
78: 
79: /// Um DEF validado, pronto para o loader.
80: pub struct Def<'a> {
81:     bytes: &'a [u8],
82:     /// Ponto de entrada virtual.
83:     pub entry: u64,
84:     /// Tamanho de pilha declarado pelo binário (0 = usar o padrão do kernel).
85:     pub stack_size: u64,
86:     /// Bitmap de capacidades concedidas (bits 0..64).
87:     pub capabilities: u64,
88:     segment_count: u32,
89: }
90: 
91: impl<'a> Def<'a> {
92:     /// Valida o cabeçalho e a tabela de segmentos.
93:     ///
94:     /// Não valida a assinatura criptográfica — isso é política do loader, que
95:     /// consulta [`Def::is_signed`] e decide o que fazer.
96:     pub fn parse(bytes: &'a [u8]) -> Result<Self, Error> {
97:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: entered\n");
98:         if bytes.len() < DEF_HEADER_SIZE {
99:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: truncated header\n");
100:             return Err(Error::Truncated);
101:         }
102:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: header size ok\n");
103:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: magic bytes: ");
104:         crate::serial_write(&bytes[..4]);
105:         crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
106:         if bytes[..4] != DEF_MAGIC {
107:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: bad magic\n");
108:             return Err(Error::InvalidMagic);
109:         }
110:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: magic ok\n");
111:         let arch = DefArch::from_u8(bytes[4]).ok_or(Error::UnsupportedArch)?;
112:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: arch ok\n");
113:         if crate::abi::def::DefType::from_u8(bytes[5]).is_none() {
114:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: invalid format type\n");
115:             return Err(Error::UnsupportedFormat);
116:         }
117:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: format type ok\n");
118:         if read_u16(bytes, 6)? != DEF_FORMAT_VERSION {
119:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: invalid format version\n");
120:             return Err(Error::UnsupportedFormat);
121:         }
122:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: format version ok\n");
123:         let abi_version = read_u32(bytes, 8)? as u64;
124:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: abi version read\n");
125:         if abi_version > crate::abi::ABI_VERSION {
126:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: ABI version mismatch\n");
127:             return Err(Error::IncompatibleAbi);
128:         }
129:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: ABI version ok\n");
130:         let segment_count = read_u32(bytes, 16)?;
131:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: segment count read\n");
132:         if segment_count == 0 || segment_count as usize > DEF_MAX_SEGMENTS {
133:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: invalid region count\n");
134:             return Err(Error::InvalidSegments);
135:         }
136:         crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: region count ok\n");
137:         let table_end = DEF_HEADER_SIZE
138:             .checked_add(segment_count as usize * DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE)
139:             .ok_or(Error::InvalidSegments)?;
140:         if table_end > bytes.len() {
141:             crate::serial_write(b"def::parse: table end out of bounds\n");
142:             return Err(Error::InvalidSegments);
143:         }
144:         let entry = read_u64(bytes, 24)?;
145:         let stack_size = read_u64(bytes, 32)?;
146:         let capabilities = read_u64(bytes, 56)?;
147:         let def = Self {
148:             bytes,
149:             entry,
150:             stack_size,
151:             capabilities,
152:             segment_count,
153:         };
154:         // Valida cada segmento: aritmética checada, dentro do arquivo, sem
155:         // W^X, e o ponto de entrada precisa cair em um segmento executável.
156:         let mut loadable = false;
157:         let mut executable_entry = false;
158:         for index in 0..segment_count as usize {
159:             if let Some(segment) = def.segment(index)? {
160:                 loadable = true;
161:                 let end = segment
162:                     .virtual_address
163:                     .checked_add(segment.memory_size)
164:                     .ok_or(Error::InvalidSegment)?;
165:                 if segment.is_executable() && entry >= segment.virtual_address && entry < end {
166:                     executable_entry = true;
167:                 }
168:             }
169:         }
170:         if !loadable || !executable_entry {
171:             return Err(Error::InvalidEntry);
172:         }
173:         Ok(def)
174:     }
175: 
176:     /// Lê a entrada `index` da tabela de segmentos.
177:     ///
178:     /// Devolve `Ok(None)` quando a entrada existe mas não é carregável (por
179:     /// exemplo, `memory_size == 0`).
180:     pub fn segment(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Option<Segment>, Error> {
181:         if index >= self.segment_count as usize {
182:             return Ok(None);
183:         }
184:         let base = DEF_HEADER_SIZE
185:             .checked_add(index * DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE)
186:             .ok_or(Error::InvalidSegments)?;
187:         let file_offset = read_u64(self.bytes, base)?;
188:         let file_size = read_u64(self.bytes, base + 8)?;
189:         let memory_size = read_u64(self.bytes, base + 16)?;
190:         let virtual_address = read_u64(self.bytes, base + 24)?;
191:         let permissions = read_u32(self.bytes, base + 32)?;
192:         let reserved = read_u32(self.bytes, base + 36)?;
193:         if memory_size == 0 {
194:             return Ok(None);
195:         }
196:         if file_size > memory_size {
197:             return Err(Error::InvalidSegment);
198:         }
199:         let file_end = file_offset
200:             .checked_add(file_size)
201:             .ok_or(Error::InvalidSegment)?;
202:         let memory_end = virtual_address
203:             .checked_add(memory_size)
204:             .ok_or(Error::InvalidSegment)?;
205:         // W^X: um segmento executável não pode ser gravável.
206:         let writable = permissions & DefPermissions::WRITE != 0;
207:         let executable = permissions & DefPermissions::EXECUTE != 0;
208:         if writable && executable {
209:             return Err(Error::InvalidSegment);
210:         }
211:         if file_end > self.bytes.len() as u64
212:             || file_offset < DEF_HEADER_SIZE as u64
213:             || virtual_address < 0x400000
214:             || memory_end > 0x0000_8000_0000_0000
215:         {
216:             return Err(Error::InvalidSegment);
217:         }
218:         let file_offset = usize::try_from(file_offset).map_err(|_| Error::InvalidSegment)?;
219:         let file_size = usize::try_from(file_size).map_err(|_| Error::InvalidSegment)?;
220:         Ok(Some(Segment {
221:             virtual_address,
222:             memory_size,
223:             file_offset,
224:             file_size,
225:             permissions,
226:             is_tls: reserved & crate::abi::def::DEF_SEGMENT_TLS != 0,
227:         }))
228:     }
229: 
230:     /// Bytes do payload de um segmento, ou `None` se a faixa for inválida.
231:     pub fn file_bytes(&self, segment: Segment) -> Option<&'a [u8]> {
232:         let end = segment.file_offset.checked_add(segment.file_size)?;
233:         self.bytes.get(segment.file_offset..end)
234:     }
235: 
236:     pub fn segment_count(&self) -> usize {
237:         self.segment_count as usize
238:     }
239: 
240:     /// Fatia de bytes do arquivo DEF.
241:     pub fn bytes(&self) -> &'a [u8] {
242:         self.bytes
243:     }
244: 
245:     pub fn is_signed(&self) -> bool {
246:         read_u32(self.bytes, 12)
247:             .map(|flags| flags & 1 != 0)
248:             .unwrap_or(false)
249:     }
250: 
251:     pub fn stack_guard(&self) -> bool {
252:         read_u32(self.bytes, 12)
253:             .map(|flags| flags & 4 != 0)
254:             .unwrap_or(false)
255:     }
256: }
257: 
258: fn read_u16(bytes: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u16, Error> {
259:     Ok(u16::from_le_bytes(
260:         bytes
261:             .get(offset..offset + 2)
262:             .ok_or(Error::Truncated)?
263:             .try_into()
264:             .map_err(|_| Error::Truncated)?,
265:     ))
266: }
267: 
268: fn read_u32(bytes: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u32, Error> {
269:     Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(
270:         bytes
271:             .get(offset..offset + 4)
272:             .ok_or(Error::Truncated)?
273:             .try_into()
274:             .map_err(|_| Error::Truncated)?,
275:     ))
276: }
277: 
278: fn read_u64(bytes: &[u8], offset: usize) -> Result<u64, Error> {
279:     Ok(u64::from_le_bytes(
280:         bytes
281:             .get(offset..offset + 8)
282:             .ok_or(Error::Truncated)?
283:             .try_into()
284:             .map_err(|_| Error::Truncated)?,
285:     ))
286: }

288: #[cfg(test)]
289: mod tests {
290:     use super::{Def, Error};
291:     use crate::abi::def::{
292:         DEF_FORMAT_VERSION, DEF_HEADER_SIZE, DEF_MAGIC, DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE, DefArch, DefPermissions,
293:         DefType,

295: 
296:     /// Monta um DEF mínimo porém válido: um único segmento de texto contendo o
297:     /// ponto de entrada.
298:     fn valid_def() -> [u8; 184] {
299:         let mut bytes = [0u8; 184];
300:         bytes[..4].copy_from_slice(&DEF_MAGIC);
301:         bytes[4] = DefArch::X86_64 as u8;
302:         bytes[5] = DefType::App as u8;
303:         bytes[6..8].copy_from_slice(&DEF_FORMAT_VERSION.to_le_bytes());
304:         bytes[8..12].copy_from_slice(&(crate::abi::ABI_VERSION as u32).to_le_bytes());
305:         bytes[16..20].copy_from_slice(&1u32.to_le_bytes()); // segment_count
306:         bytes[24..32].copy_from_slice(&0x400000u64.to_le_bytes()); // entry_point
307:         bytes[32..40].copy_from_slice(&(32u64 * 4096).to_le_bytes()); // stack_size
308:         bytes[40..48].copy_from_slice(&0x400000u64.to_le_bytes()); // image_base
309:         bytes[48..56].copy_from_slice(&0x1000u64.to_le_bytes()); // image_size
310: 
311:         let base = DEF_HEADER_SIZE;
312:         bytes[base..base + 8].copy_from_slice(&152u64.to_le_bytes()); // file_offset
313:         bytes[base + 8..base + 16].copy_from_slice(&8u64.to_le_bytes()); // file_size
314:         bytes[base + 16..base + 24].copy_from_slice(&0x1000u64.to_le_bytes()); // memory_size
315:         bytes[base + 24..base + 32].copy_from_slice(&0x400000u64.to_le_bytes()); // vaddr
316:         bytes[base + 32..base + 36].copy_from_slice(&DefPermissions::TEXT.to_le_bytes()); // perms
317:         let payload = DEF_HEADER_SIZE + DEF_SEGMENT_SIZE;
318:         bytes[payload..payload + 8].copy_from_slice(b"testcode");
319:         bytes
320:     }
321: 
322:     #[test]
323:     fn parses_valid_def() {
324:         let bytes = valid_def();
325:         let def = Def::parse(&bytes).expect("valid def");
326:         assert_eq!(def.entry, 0x400000);
327:         assert_eq!(def.segment_count(), 1);
328:         let segment = def.segment(0).expect("segment").expect("loadable");
329:         assert_eq!(segment.virtual_address, 0x400000);
330:         assert!(segment.is_executable());
331:         assert!(!segment.is_writable());
332:         assert_eq!(def.file_bytes(segment).unwrap(), b"testcode");
333:     }
334: 
335:     #[test]
336:     fn rejects_bad_magic_and_truncation() {
337:         assert!(matches!(Def::parse(&[0u8; 8]), Err(Error::Truncated)));
338:         let mut bytes = valid_def();
339:         bytes[0] = b'X';
340:         assert!(matches!(Def::parse(&bytes), Err(Error::InvalidMagic)));
341:     }
342: 
343:     #[test]
344:     fn rejects_wxor_writable_executable_segment() {
345:         let mut bytes = valid_def();
346:         let base = DEF_HEADER_SIZE;
347:         let rwx = DefPermissions::READ | DefPermissions::WRITE | DefPermissions::EXECUTE;
348:         bytes[base + 32..base + 36].copy_from_slice(&rwx.to_le_bytes());
349:         assert!(matches!(Def::parse(&bytes), Err(Error::InvalidSegment)));
350:     }
351: 
352:     #[test]
353:     fn rejects_entry_outside_executable_segment() {
354:         let mut bytes = valid_def();
355:         bytes[24..32].copy_from_slice(&0xdead_beefu64.to_le_bytes());
356:         assert!(matches!(Def::parse(&bytes), Err(Error::InvalidEntry)));
357:     }
358: 
359:     #[test]
360:     fn rejects_newer_abi() {
361:         let mut bytes = valid_def();
362:         let abi = (crate::abi::ABI_VERSION as u32) + 1;
363:         bytes[8..12].copy_from_slice(&abi.to_le_bytes());
364:         assert!(matches!(Def::parse(&bytes), Err(Error::IncompatibleAbi)));
365:     }
366: 
367:     #[test]

369:         let mut bytes = valid_def();
370:         let base = DEF_HEADER_SIZE;
371:         bytes[base..base + 8].copy_from_slice(&u64::MAX.to_le_bytes()); // file_offset

172:     }
173: 
174:     fn metadata(&self, wanted: &str) -> Option<crate::dfs::Metadata> {
175:         let wanted = wanted.trim_start_matches('/');
176:         let mut offset = 0usize;
177:         while offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)? <= self.bytes.len() {
178:             let header = &self.bytes[offset..offset + BLOCK_SIZE];
179:             if header.iter().all(|byte| *byte == 0) {
180:                 return None;
181:             }
182:             let name = field(&header[..100]);
183:             let prefix = field(&header[345..500]);
184:             let mut path = [0u8; 256];
185:             let mut path_length = if prefix.is_empty() {
186:                 path[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
187:                 name.len()
188:             } else {
189:                 let length = prefix.len().checked_add(1)?.checked_add(name.len())?;
190:                 if length > path.len() {
191:                     return None;
192:                 }
193:                 path[..prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
194:                 path[prefix.len()] = b'/';
195:                 path[prefix.len() + 1..length].copy_from_slice(name);
196:                 length
197:             };
198:             while path_length != 0 && path[path_length - 1] == b'/' {
199:                 path_length -= 1;
200:             }
201:             if &path[..path_length] == wanted.as_bytes() {
202:                 return Some(crate::dfs::Metadata {
203:                     size: parse_octal(&header[124..136])? as u64,
204:                     mode: parse_octal(&header[100..108]).unwrap_or(0o644) as u16,
205:                     uid: parse_octal(&header[108..116]).unwrap_or(0) as u32,
206:                     gid: parse_octal(&header[116..124]).unwrap_or(0) as u32,
207:                     is_directory: header[156] == b'5',
208:                 });
209:             }
210:             let size = parse_octal(&header[124..136])?;
211:             let padded_size = size.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE - 1)? & !(BLOCK_SIZE - 1);
212:             offset = offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)?.checked_add(padded_size)?;
213:         }
214:         None
215:     }
216: 
217:     pub fn find_font(&self) -> Option<&'a [u8]> {
218:         let mut offset = 0usize;
219:         while offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)? <= self.bytes.len() {
220:             let header = &self.bytes[offset..offset + BLOCK_SIZE];
221:             if header.iter().all(|byte| *byte == 0) {
222:                 return None;
223:             }
224:             let name = field(&header[..100]);
225:             let prefix = field(&header[345..500]);
226:             let mut path = [0u8; 256];
227:             let path_len = if prefix.is_empty() {
228:                 if name.len() > path.len() {
229:                     return None;
230:                 }
231:                 path[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
232:                 name.len()
233:             } else {
234:                 let required = prefix.len().checked_add(1)?.checked_add(name.len())?;
235:                 if required > path.len() {
236:                     return None;
237:                 }
238:                 path[..prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
239:                 path[prefix.len()] = b'/';
240:                 path[prefix.len() + 1..required].copy_from_slice(name);
241:                 required
242:             };
243:             let path = &path[..path_len];
244:             let in_font_directory = path.starts_with(b"system/fonts/");
245:             let supported_font = path.ends_with(b".psf") || path.ends_with(b".psf2");
246:             let size = parse_octal(&header[124..136])?;
247:             let data_start = offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)?;
248:             let data_end = data_start.checked_add(size)?;
249:             if data_end > self.bytes.len() {
250:                 return None;
251:             }
252:             if in_font_directory && supported_font && (header[156] == 0 || header[156] == b'0') {
253:                 return Some(&self.bytes[data_start..data_end]);
254:             }
255:             let padded_size = size.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE - 1)? & !(BLOCK_SIZE - 1);
256:             offset = data_start.checked_add(padded_size)?;
257:         }
258:         None
259:     }
260: 
261:     pub fn is_directory(&self, wanted: &str) -> bool {
262:         let wanted = wanted.trim_start_matches('/');
263:         if wanted.is_empty() {
264:             return true;
265:         }
266:         let mut offset = 0usize;
267:         while offset
268:             .checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)
269:             .is_some_and(|end| end <= self.bytes.len())
270:         {
271:             let header = &self.bytes[offset..offset + BLOCK_SIZE];
272:             if header.iter().all(|byte| *byte == 0) {
273:                 return false;
274:             }
275:             let name = field(&header[..100]);
276:             let prefix = field(&header[345..500]);
277:             let mut path = [0u8; 256];
278:             let path_len = if prefix.is_empty() {
279:                 if name.len() > path.len() {
280:                     return false;
281:                 }
282:                 path[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
283:                 name.len()
284:             } else {
285:                 let Some(required) = prefix
286:                     .len()
287:                     .checked_add(1)
288:                     .and_then(|n| n.checked_add(name.len()))
289:                 else {
290:                     return false;
291:                 };
292:                 if required > path.len() {
293:                     return false;
294:                 }
295:                 path[..prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
296:                 path[prefix.len()] = b'/';
297:                 path[prefix.len() + 1..required].copy_from_slice(name);
298:                 required
299:             };
300:             let current = &path[..path_len];
301:             if (current == wanted.as_bytes() && header[156] == b'5')
302:                 || current
303:                     .strip_prefix(wanted.as_bytes())
304:                     .is_some_and(|rest| rest.starts_with(b"/"))
305:             {
306:                 return true;
307:             }
308:             let Some(size) = parse_octal(&header[124..136]) else {
309:                 return false;
310:             };
311:             let Some(data_start) = offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE) else {
312:                 return false;
313:             };
314:             let Some(padded_size) = size
315:                 .checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE - 1)
316:                 .map(|size| size & !(BLOCK_SIZE - 1))
317:             else {
318:                 return false;
319:             };
320:             let Some(next) = data_start.checked_add(padded_size) else {
321:                 return false;
322:             };
323:             if next > self.bytes.len() {
324:                 return false;
325:             }
326:             offset = next;
327:         }
328:         false
329:     }
330: 
331:     pub fn is_file(&self, wanted: &str) -> bool {
332:         self.find(wanted).is_some()
333:     }
334: 
335:     pub fn list_directory(&self, directory: &str, output: &mut [u8]) -> Option<usize> {
336:         let wanted = directory.trim_matches('/');
337:         let mut offset = 0usize;
338:         let mut written = 0usize;
339:         while offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)? <= self.bytes.len() {
340:             let header = &self.bytes[offset..offset + BLOCK_SIZE];
341:             if header.iter().all(|byte| *byte == 0) {
342:                 break;
343:             }
344:             let name = field(&header[..100]);
345:             let prefix = field(&header[345..500]);
346:             let mut full_path = [0u8; 256];
347:             let path_length = if prefix.is_empty() {
348:                 if name.len() > full_path.len() {
349:                     return None;
350:                 }
351:                 full_path[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
352:                 name.len()
353:             } else {
354:                 let required = prefix.len().checked_add(1)?.checked_add(name.len())?;
355:                 if required > full_path.len() {
356:                     return None;
357:                 }
358:                 full_path[..prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
359:                 full_path[prefix.len()] = b'/';
360:                 full_path[prefix.len() + 1..required].copy_from_slice(name);
361:                 required
362:             };
363:             let full_path = &full_path[..path_length];
364:             let relative = if wanted.is_empty() {
365:                 Some(full_path)
366:             } else {
367:                 full_path
368:                     .strip_prefix(wanted.as_bytes())
369:                     .and_then(|suffix| suffix.strip_prefix(b"/"))
370:             };
371:             if let Some(relative) = relative {
372:                 if let Some(component) = relative.split(|byte| *byte == b'/').next() {
373:                     if !component.is_empty() {
374:                         let mut already_listed = false;
375:                         for existing in output[..written].split(|byte| *byte == b'\n') {
376:                             if existing == component {
377:                                 already_listed = true;
378:                                 break;
379:                             }
380:                         }
381:                         if !already_listed {
382:                             let required = written.checked_add(component.len())?.checked_add(1)?;
383:                             if required > output.len() {
384:                                 return None;
385:                             }
386:                             output[written..written + component.len()].copy_from_slice(component);
387:                             output[required - 1] = b'\n';
388:                             written = required;
389:                         }
390:                     }
391:                 }
392:             }
393:             let size = parse_octal(&header[124..136])?;
394:             let data_start = offset.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE)?;
395:             let padded_size = size.checked_add(BLOCK_SIZE - 1)? & !(BLOCK_SIZE - 1);
396:             offset = data_start.checked_add(padded_size)?;
397:         }
398:         Some(written)
399:     }
400: }
401: 
402: struct SharedRamFs(UnsafeCell<RamFs>);
403: unsafe impl Sync for SharedRamFs {}
404: #[unsafe(link_section = ".data.early")]
405: static MOUNTED_RAMFS: SharedRamFs = SharedRamFs(UnsafeCell::new(RamFs::new()));
406: static BLOCK_DISK_PRESENT: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
407: 
408: pub fn set_block_disk_present(present: bool) {
409:     BLOCK_DISK_PRESENT.store(present, Ordering::Relaxed);
410: }
411: 
412: fn block_disk_present() -> bool {
413:     BLOCK_DISK_PRESENT.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
414: }
415: 
416: pub fn mount(bytes: &'static [u8]) {
417:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
418:     fs.archive = Some(Archive::new(bytes));
419:     fs.overlay = [OverlayNode::EMPTY; MAX_OVERLAY_NODES];
420:     fs.file_data_used = 0;
421: }
422: 
423: pub fn read(path: &str) -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
424:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().read_static(path)
425: }
426: 
427: /// Reads a whole file using only the USTAR archive plus the in-memory overlay.
428: ///
429: /// This is the backend body used by [`crate::vfs`]; it never consults the DFS
430: /// root, and every helper it calls is an `*_overlay` variant for that reason.
431: pub fn read_from_overlay(path: &str) -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
432:     crate::serial_write(b"ramfs::read_from_overlay(\"");
433:     crate::serial_write(path.as_bytes());
434:     crate::serial_write(b")\r\n");
435:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
436:     crate::serial_write(b"ramfs::read_from_overlay: fs.archive=");
437:     crate::serial_write(if fs.archive.is_some() { b"some" } else { b"none" });
438:     crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
439:     let path = canonical_path(path)?;
440:     if is_hidden(fs, path) {
441:         return None;
442:     }
443:     match find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.kind) {
444:         Some(NodeKind::File) => {
445:             let node = find_overlay_node(fs, path)?;
446:             Some(&fs.file_data[node.data_start..node.data_start + node.data_length])
447:         }
448:         Some(NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory | NodeKind::Whiteout) => None,
449:         Some(NodeKind::Empty) | None => fs.archive.as_ref()?.find(path),
450:     }
451: }
452: 
453: pub fn read_file_into(path: &str, output: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
454:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
455:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
456:     if path.is_empty() || is_hidden(fs, path) {
457:         return Err(FsError::NotFound);
458:     }
459:     let contents = match find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.kind) {
460:         Some(NodeKind::File) => {
461:             let node = find_overlay_node(fs, path).ok_or(FsError::NotFound)?;
462:             &fs.file_data[node.data_start..node.data_start + node.data_length]
463:         }
464:         Some(NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory | NodeKind::Whiteout) => {
465:             return Err(FsError::NotFound);
466:         }
467:         Some(NodeKind::Empty) | None => fs
468:             .archive
469:             .as_ref()
470:             .and_then(|archive| archive.find(path))
471:             .ok_or(FsError::NotFound)?,
472:     };
473:     if contents.len() > output.len() {
474:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
475:     }
476:     output[..contents.len()].copy_from_slice(contents);
477:     Ok(contents.len())
478: }
479: 
480: pub fn file_size(path: &str) -> Option<usize> {
481:     let path = canonical_path(path)?;
482:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
483:     if is_hidden(fs, path) {
484:         return None;
485:     }
486:     match find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.kind) {
487:         Some(NodeKind::File) => find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.data_length),
488:         Some(NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory | NodeKind::Whiteout) => None,
489:         Some(NodeKind::Empty) | None => fs.archive.as_ref()?.find(path).map(|bytes| bytes.len()),
490:     }
491: }
492: 
493: pub fn find_font() -> Option<&'static [u8]> {
494:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
495:     if crate::vfs::has_persistent_root() {
496:         let mut entries = [0u8; 4096];
497:         let length = crate::dfs::list_directory("system/fonts", false, &mut entries).ok()?;
498:         for name in entries[..length].split(|byte| *byte == b'\n') {
499:             if name.ends_with(b".psf") || name.ends_with(b".psf2") {
500:                 let mut path = [0u8; 256];
501:                 let prefix = b"system/fonts/";
502:                 let total = prefix.len().checked_add(name.len())?;
503:                 if total > path.len() {
504:                     continue;
505:                 }
506:                 path[..prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
507:                 path[prefix.len()..total].copy_from_slice(name);
508:                 if let Ok(path) = core::str::from_utf8(&path[..total]) {
509:                     return crate::dfs::read_static(path);
510:                 }
511:             }
512:         }
513:         return None;
514:     }
515:     fs.archive.as_ref()?.find_font()
516: }
517: 
518: pub fn is_directory(path: &str) -> bool {
519:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().is_directory(path)
520: }
521: 
522: /// Backend-specific `is_directory` over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
523: pub fn is_directory_overlay(path: &str) -> bool {
524:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
525:     let Some(path) = canonical_path(path) else {
526:         return false;
527:     };
528:     if path == "dev" {
529:         return true;
530:     }
531:     match find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.kind) {
532:         Some(NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory) => return true,
533:         Some(NodeKind::File | NodeKind::Whiteout) => return false,
534:         Some(NodeKind::Empty) | None => {}
535:     }
536:     if is_hidden(fs, path) {
537:         return false;
538:     }
539:     fs.archive
540:         .as_ref()
541:         .is_some_and(|archive| archive.is_directory(path))
542:         || fs.overlay.iter().any(|node| {
543:             matches!(
544:                 node.kind,
545:                 NodeKind::File | NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory
546:             ) && is_child_path(node.path(), path)
547:         })
548: }
549: 
550: pub fn is_file(path: &str) -> bool {
551:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().is_file(path)
552: }
553: 
554: /// Backend-specific `is_file` over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
555: pub fn is_file_overlay(path: &str) -> bool {
556:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
557:     let Some(path) = canonical_path(path) else {
558:         return false;
559:     };
560:     if path == "dev/hda" {
561:         return block_disk_present();
562:     }
563:     if is_hidden(fs, path) {
564:         return false;
565:     }
566:     match find_overlay_node(fs, path).map(|node| node.kind) {
567:         Some(NodeKind::File) => true,
568:         Some(NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory | NodeKind::Whiteout) => false,
569:         Some(NodeKind::Empty) | None => fs
570:             .archive
571:             .as_ref()
572:             .is_some_and(|archive| archive.is_file(path)),
573:     }
574: }
575: 
576: pub fn metadata(path: &str) -> Option<crate::dfs::Metadata> {
577:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().metadata(path)
578: }
579: 
580: /// Backend-specific `metadata` over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
581: pub fn metadata_overlay(path: &str) -> Option<crate::dfs::Metadata> {
582:     let path = canonical_path(path)?;
583:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
584:     if let Some(node) = find_overlay_node(fs, path) {
585:         return Some(crate::dfs::Metadata {
586:             size: node.data_length as u64,
587:             mode: node.mode,
588:             uid: node.uid,
589:             gid: node.gid,
590:             is_directory: matches!(node.kind, NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory),
591:         });
592:     }
593:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
594:         if let Some(metadata) = fs
595:             .archive
596:             .as_ref()
597:             .and_then(|archive| archive.metadata(path))
598:         {
599:             return Some(metadata);
600:         }
601:         return Some(crate::dfs::Metadata {
602:             size: 0,
603:             mode: 0o755,
604:             uid: 0,
605:             gid: 0,
606:             is_directory: true,
607:         });
608:     }
609:     if path == "dev/hda" && block_disk_present() {
610:         return Some(crate::dfs::Metadata {
611:             size: crate::ata::sector_count() * BLOCK_SIZE as u64,
612:             mode: 0o600,
613:             uid: 0,
614:             gid: 0,
615:             is_directory: false,
616:         });
617:     }
618:     let size = file_len_overlay(path).ok()?;
619:     if let Some(metadata) = fs
620:         .archive
621:         .as_ref()
622:         .and_then(|archive| archive.metadata(path))
623:     {
624:         return Some(metadata);
625:     }
626:     Some(crate::dfs::Metadata {
627:         size: size as u64,
628:         mode: 0o644,
629:         uid: 0,
630:         gid: 0,
631:         is_directory: false,
632:     })
633: }
634: 
635: pub fn list_directory(path: &str, output: &mut [u8]) -> Option<usize> {
636:     list_directory_with_hidden(path, false, output)
637: }
638: 
639: pub fn list_directory_with_hidden(
640:     path: &str,
641:     include_hidden: bool,
642:     output: &mut [u8],
643: ) -> Option<usize> {
644:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().list_directory(path, include_hidden, output)
645: }
646: 
647: /// Backend-specific directory listing over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
648: pub fn list_directory_overlay(
649:     path: &str,
650:     include_hidden: bool,
651:     output: &mut [u8],
652: ) -> Option<usize> {
653:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
654:     let path = canonical_path(path)?;
655:     if !is_directory_overlay(path) {
656:         return None;
657:     }
658:     if path == "dev" {
659:         if block_disk_present() {
660:             let mut written = 0;
661:             append_name(output, &mut written, b"hda")?;
662:             return Some(written);
663:         }
664:         return Some(0);
665:     }
666:     let opaque =
667:         find_overlay_node(fs, path).is_some_and(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory);
668:     let mut entries = [0u8; 4096];
669:     let mut written = 0usize;
670:     if !opaque {
671:         if let Some(archive) = fs.archive.as_ref() {
672:             if archive.is_directory(path) {
673:                 if let Some(base_length) = archive.list_directory(path, &mut entries) {
674:                     for name in entries[..base_length].split(|byte| *byte == b'\n') {
675:                         if name.is_empty() {
676:                             continue;
677:                         }
678:                         let mut child = [0u8; 256];
679:                         let child_length = join_child(path, name, &mut child)?;
680:                         let child_path = core::str::from_utf8(&child[..child_length]).ok()?;
681:                         let basename = name.rsplit(|byte| *byte == b'/').next().unwrap_or(name);
682:                         if !is_hidden(fs, child_path)
683:                             && (include_hidden || !basename.starts_with(b"."))
684:                         {
685:                             append_name(output, &mut written, name)?;
686:                         }
687:                     }
688:                 }
689:             }
690:         }
691:     }
692:     for node in fs.overlay.iter().filter(|node| {
693:         matches!(
694:             node.kind,
695:             NodeKind::File | NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory
696:         )
697:     }) {
698:         if let Some(Some(name)) = immediate_child(node.path(), path) {
699:             if include_hidden || !name.starts_with(b".") {
700:                 append_name(output, &mut written, name)?;
701:             }
702:         }
703:     }
704:     Some(written)
705: }
706: 
707: pub fn create_file(path: &str) -> Result<(), FsError> {
708:     create_file_as(path, 0, 0)
709: }
710: 
711: pub fn create_file_as(path: &str, uid: u32, gid: u32) -> Result<(), FsError> {
712:     create_file_with_mode_as(path, 0o644, uid, gid)
713: }
714: 
715: pub fn create_file_with_mode_as(
716:     path: &str,
717:     mode: u16,
718:     uid: u32,
719:     gid: u32,
720: ) -> Result<(), FsError> {
721:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
722:         .create_file_with_mode_as(path, mode, uid, gid)
723:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
724: }
725: 
726: /// Backend-specific file creation over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
727: pub fn create_file_with_mode_overlay(
728:     path: &str,
729:     mode: u16,
730:     uid: u32,
731:     gid: u32,
732: ) -> Result<(), FsError> {
733:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
734:     if path == "dev" || path == "dev/hda" {
735:         return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
736:     }
737:     if path.is_empty() {
738:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
739:     }
740:     if is_file_overlay(path) {
741:         return Ok(());
742:     }
743:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
744:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
745:     }
746:     ensure_parent_directory(path)?;
747:     insert_overlay_as(path, NodeKind::File, mode & 0o777, uid, gid)
748: }
749: 
750: pub fn write_file_as(path: &str, bytes: &[u8], uid: u32, gid: u32) -> Result<(), FsError> {
751:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
752:         .write_file_as(path, bytes, uid, gid)
753:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
754: }
755: 
756: /// Backend-specific whole-file write over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
757: pub fn write_file_overlay(path: &str, bytes: &[u8], uid: u32, gid: u32) -> Result<(), FsError> {
758:     if bytes.len() > MAX_WRITE_FILE_SIZE {
759:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
760:     }
761:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
762:     if path == "dev" || path == "dev/hda" {
763:         return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
764:     }
765:     if path.is_empty() {
766:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
767:     }
768:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
769:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
770:     }
771:     if !is_file_overlay(path) {
772:         ensure_parent_directory(path)?;
773:     }
774:     let old_metadata = metadata_overlay(path);
775:     let (mode, owner_uid, owner_gid) = old_metadata.map_or((0o644, uid, gid), |metadata| {
776:         (metadata.mode, metadata.uid, metadata.gid)
777:     });
778:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
779:     let existing_slot = fs
780:         .overlay
781:         .iter()
782:         .position(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::File && node.path() == path);
783:     let old_capacity = existing_slot.map_or(0, |slot| fs.overlay[slot].data_capacity);
784:     let reusable = existing_slot.is_some() && bytes.len() <= old_capacity;
785:     let data_capacity = if reusable {
786:         old_capacity
787:     } else if bytes.is_empty() {
788:         0
789:     } else {
790:         old_capacity.saturating_mul(2).max(bytes.len()).max(4096)
791:     };
792:     if data_capacity > OVERLAY_DATA_CAPACITY - fs.file_data_used {
793:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
794:     }
795:     let data_start = if reusable {
796:         fs.overlay[existing_slot.expect("reusable overlay node")].data_start
797:     } else {
798:         let start = fs.file_data_used;
799:         let end = start + data_capacity;
800:         fs.file_data[start..start + bytes.len()].copy_from_slice(bytes);
801:         fs.file_data_used = end;
802:         start
803:     };
804:     if reusable {
805:         fs.file_data[data_start..data_start + bytes.len()].copy_from_slice(bytes);
806:     }
807:     insert_overlay_as(path, NodeKind::File, mode, owner_uid, owner_gid)?;
808:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
809:     let node = fs
810:         .overlay
811:         .iter_mut()
812:         .find(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::File && node.path() == path)
813:         .ok_or(FsError::NoSpace)?;
814:     node.data_start = data_start;
815:     node.data_length = bytes.len();
816:     node.data_capacity = data_capacity;
817:     Ok(())
818: }
819: 
820: pub fn file_len(path: &str) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
821:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current().file_len(path).map_err(map_vfs_error)
822: }
823: 
824: /// Backend-specific length query over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
825: pub fn file_len_overlay(path: &str) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
826:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
827:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
828:     }
829:     read_from_overlay(path)
830:         .map(|contents| contents.len())
831:         .ok_or(FsError::NotFound)
832: }
833: 
834: pub fn read_at(path: &str, offset: usize, output: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
835:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
836:         .read_at(path, offset, output)
837:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
838: }
839: 
840: /// Backend-specific ranged read over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
841: pub fn read_at_overlay(path: &str, offset: usize, output: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
842:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
843:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
844:     }
845:     let contents = read_from_overlay(path).ok_or(FsError::NotFound)?;
846:     if offset >= contents.len() {
847:         return Ok(0);
848:     }
849:     let length = output.len().min(contents.len() - offset);
850:     output[..length].copy_from_slice(&contents[offset..offset + length]);
851:     Ok(length)
852: }
853: 
854: pub fn write_at(path: &str, offset: usize, input: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
855:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
856:         .write_at(path, offset, input)
857:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
858: }
859: 
860: /// Backend-specific ranged write over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
861: pub fn write_at_overlay(path: &str, offset: usize, input: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, FsError> {
862:     let end = offset.checked_add(input.len()).ok_or(FsError::NoSpace)?;
863:     if end > MAX_OVERLAY_FILE_SIZE {
864:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
865:     }
866:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
867:     if path.is_empty() || is_directory_overlay(path) {
868:         return Err(FsError::IsDirectory);
869:     }
870:     let original = read_from_overlay(path).ok_or(FsError::NotFound)?;
871:     if offset > original.len() {
872:         return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
873:     }
874:     let original_length = original.len();
875:     let new_length = original_length.max(end);
876:     if new_length > MAX_OVERLAY_FILE_SIZE {
877:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
878:     }
879:     ensure_parent_directory(path)?;
880:     let metadata = metadata_overlay(path);
881:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
882:     let existing_slot = fs
883:         .overlay
884:         .iter()
885:         .position(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::File && node.path() == path);
886:     let old_capacity = existing_slot.map_or(0, |slot| fs.overlay[slot].data_capacity);
887:     let reusable = existing_slot.is_some() && new_length <= old_capacity;
888:     let data_capacity = if reusable {
889:         old_capacity
890:     } else if new_length == 0 {
891:         0
892:     } else {
893:         old_capacity
894:             .saturating_mul(2)
895:             .max(new_length)
896:             .max(4096)
897:             .min(MAX_OVERLAY_FILE_SIZE)
898:     };
899:     if data_capacity < new_length
900:         || data_capacity > OVERLAY_DATA_CAPACITY - fs.file_data_used
901:     {
902:         return Err(FsError::NoSpace);
903:     }
904:     let data_start = if reusable {
905:         fs.overlay[existing_slot.expect("reusable overlay node")].data_start
906:     } else {
907:         let start = fs.file_data_used;
908:         let end = start + data_capacity;
909:         fs.file_data[start..start + original_length].copy_from_slice(original);
910:         fs.file_data_used = end;
911:         start
912:     };
913:     fs.file_data[data_start + offset..data_start + end].copy_from_slice(input);
914:     let slot = existing_slot
915:         .or_else(|| {
916:             fs.overlay
917:                 .iter()
918:                 .position(|node| node.kind != NodeKind::Empty && node.path() == path)
919:         })
920:         .or_else(|| {
921:             fs.overlay
922:                 .iter()
923:                 .position(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::Empty)
924:         })
925:         .ok_or(FsError::NoSpace)?;
926:     let mut node = fs.overlay[slot];
927:     node.path.fill(0);
928:     node.path[..path.len()].copy_from_slice(path.as_bytes());
929:     node.path_length = path.len();
930:     node.kind = NodeKind::File;
931:     node.data_start = data_start;
932:     node.data_length = new_length;
933:     node.data_capacity = data_capacity;
934:     node.mode = metadata.map_or(0o644, |metadata| metadata.mode);
935:     node.uid = metadata.map_or(0, |metadata| metadata.uid);
936:     node.gid = metadata.map_or(0, |metadata| metadata.gid);
937:     fs.overlay[slot] = node;
938:     Ok(input.len())
939: }
940: 
941: /// Writes a whole file, creating it when absent.
942: ///
943: /// This used to be a second, near-identical copy of [`write_file_as`] with a
944: /// different size ceiling (`MAX_OVERLAY_FILE_SIZE` here versus
945: /// `MAX_WRITE_FILE_SIZE` there), so the same call could succeed or fail
946: /// depending on which one the caller happened to reach. It now routes through
947: /// the VFS like every other mutation, which gives it one consistent limit.
948: pub fn write_file(path: &str, contents: &[u8]) -> Result<(), FsError> {
949:     write_file_as(path, contents, 0, 0)
950: }
951: 
952: pub fn create_directory(path: &str) -> Result<(), FsError> {
953:     create_directory_with_parents(path, false)
954: }
955: 
956: pub fn create_directory_as(path: &str, uid: u32, gid: u32) -> Result<(), FsError> {
957:     // A single-level directory is just the `parents = false` case of the routed
958:     // entry point, so it goes through the VFS like every other mutation.
959:     create_directory_with_parents_as(path, false, uid, gid)
960: }
961: 
962: pub fn create_directory_with_parents(path: &str, parents: bool) -> Result<(), FsError> {
963:     create_directory_with_parents_as(path, parents, 0, 0)
964: }
965: 
966: pub fn create_directory_with_parents_as(
967:     path: &str,
968:     parents: bool,
969:     uid: u32,
970:     gid: u32,
971: ) -> Result<(), FsError> {
972:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
973:         .create_directory_with_parents_as(path, parents, uid, gid)
974:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
975: }
976: 
977: /// Backend-specific directory creation over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
978: pub fn create_directory_with_parents_overlay(
979:     path: &str,
980:     parents: bool,
981:     uid: u32,
982:     gid: u32,
983: ) -> Result<(), FsError> {
984:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
985:     if path.is_empty() {
986:         return Err(FsError::AlreadyExists);
987:     }
988:     if parents {
989:         let mut current = [0u8; 256];
990:         let mut current_length = 0usize;
991:         for component in path.split('/') {
992:             if current_length != 0 {
993:                 current[current_length] = b'/';
994:                 current_length += 1;
995:             }
996:             if current_length + component.len() > current.len() {
997:                 return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
998:             }
999:             current[current_length..current_length + component.len()]
1000:                 .copy_from_slice(component.as_bytes());
1001:             current_length += component.len();
1002:             let current_path = core::str::from_utf8(&current[..current_length])
1003:                 .map_err(|_| FsError::InvalidPath)?;
1004:             if is_directory_overlay(current_path) {
1005:                 continue;
1006:             }
1007:             if is_file_overlay(current_path) {
1008:                 return Err(FsError::AlreadyExists);
1009:             }
1010:             create_directory_one_as(current_path, uid, gid)?;
1011:         }
1012:         return Ok(());
1013:     }
1014:     create_directory_one_as(path, uid, gid)
1015: }
1016: 
1017: fn create_directory_one(path: &str) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1018:     create_directory_one_as(path, 0, 0)
1019: }
1020: 
1021: fn create_directory_one_as(path: &str, uid: u32, gid: u32) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1022:     if is_file_overlay(path) || is_directory_overlay(path) {
1023:         return Err(FsError::AlreadyExists);
1024:     }
1025:     ensure_parent_directory(path)?;
1026:     let fs = unsafe { &*MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
1027:     let hides_archive = fs
1028:         .archive
1029:         .as_ref()
1030:         .is_some_and(|archive| archive.is_directory(path));
1031:     insert_overlay_as(
1032:         path,
1033:         if hides_archive {
1034:             NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory
1035:         } else {
1036:             NodeKind::Directory
1037:         },
1038:         0o755,
1039:         uid,
1040:         gid,
1041:     )
1042: }
1043: 
1044: pub fn remove(path: &str, recursive: bool) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1045:     crate::vfs::Vfs::current()
1046:         .remove(path, recursive)
1047:         .map_err(map_vfs_error)
1048: }
1049: 
1050: /// Backend-specific removal over the USTAR archive and overlay only.
1051: pub fn remove_overlay(path: &str, recursive: bool) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1052:     let path = canonical_path(path).ok_or(FsError::InvalidPath)?;
1053:     if path.is_empty() || path == "dev" || path == "dev/hda" {
1054:         return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
1055:     }
1056: 
1057:     if !is_file_overlay(path) && !is_directory_overlay(path) {
1058:         return Err(FsError::NotFound);
1059:     }
1060:     if is_directory_overlay(path) {
1061:         let mut children = [0u8; 4096];
1062:         if list_directory_overlay(path, false, &mut children).unwrap_or(0) != 0 && !recursive {
1063:             return Err(FsError::DirectoryNotEmpty);
1064:         }
1065:     }
1066:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
1067:     let exists_in_archive = fs
1068:         .archive
1069:         .as_ref()
1070:         .is_some_and(|archive| archive.is_file(path) || archive.is_directory(path));
1071:     if exists_in_archive {
1072:         insert_overlay(path, NodeKind::Whiteout)?;
1073:     } else {
1074:         for node in fs.overlay.iter_mut() {
1075:             if node.kind != NodeKind::Empty
1076:                 && (node.path() == path || is_child_path(node.path(), path))
1077:             {
1078:                 *node = OverlayNode::EMPTY;
1079:             }
1080:         }
1081:     }
1082:     Ok(())
1083: }
1084: 
1085: fn map_vfs_error(error: crate::vfs::VfsError) -> FsError {
1086:     use crate::vfs::VfsError;
1087:     match error {
1088:         VfsError::InvalidPath => FsError::InvalidPath,
1089:         VfsError::NotFound => FsError::NotFound,
1090:         VfsError::NotDirectory => FsError::NotDirectory,
1091:         VfsError::IsDirectory => FsError::IsDirectory,
1092:         VfsError::AlreadyExists => FsError::AlreadyExists,
1093:         VfsError::DirectoryNotEmpty => FsError::DirectoryNotEmpty,
1094:         VfsError::NoSpace => FsError::NoSpace,
1095:         // Read-only and I/O failures have no dedicated FsError variant; the
1096:         // facade reports them as capacity errors, matching the previous DFS
1097:         // mapping behaviour.
1098:         VfsError::Io => FsError::NoSpace,
1099:     }
1100: }
1101: 
1102: fn find_overlay_node<'a>(fs: &'a RamFs, path: &str) -> Option<&'a OverlayNode> {
1103:     fs.overlay
1104:         .iter()
1105:         .find(|node| node.kind != NodeKind::Empty && node.path() == path)
1106: }
1107: 
1108: fn is_hidden(fs: &RamFs, path: &str) -> bool {
1109:     if let Some(node) = find_overlay_node(fs, path) {
1110:         return node.kind == NodeKind::Whiteout;
1111:     }
1112:     fs.overlay.iter().any(|node| {
1113:         (node.kind == NodeKind::Whiteout
1114:             && (node.path() == path || is_child_path(path, node.path())))
1115:             || (node.kind == NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory && is_child_path(path, node.path()))
1116:     })
1117: }
1118: 
1119: fn is_child_path(path: &str, parent: &str) -> bool {
1120:     if parent.is_empty() {
1121:         return !path.is_empty();
1122:     }
1123:     path.strip_prefix(parent)
1124:         .is_some_and(|suffix| suffix.starts_with('/'))
1125: }
1126: 
1127: fn canonical_path(path: &str) -> Option<&str> {
1128:     if path.is_empty() {
1129:         return Some(path);
1130:     }
1131:     let path = path.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or(path);
1132:     if path.is_empty() {
1133:         return Some(path);
1134:     }
1135:     if path
1136:         .split('/')
1137:         .any(|component| component.is_empty() || component == "." || component == "..")
1138:     {
1139:         return None;
1140:     }
1141:     Some(path)
1142: }
1143: 
1144: fn ensure_parent_directory(path: &str) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1145:     let parent = path.rsplit_once('/').map_or("", |(parent, _)| parent);
1146:     if is_directory_overlay(parent) {
1147:         Ok(())
1148:     } else {
1149:         Err(FsError::NotDirectory)
1150:     }
1151: }
1152: 
1153: fn insert_overlay(path: &str, kind: NodeKind) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1154:     insert_overlay_as(
1155:         path,
1156:         kind,
1157:         if matches!(kind, NodeKind::Directory | NodeKind::OpaqueDirectory) {
1158:             0o755
1159:         } else {
1160:             0o644
1161:         },
1162:         0,
1163:         0,
1164:     )
1165: }
1166: 
1167: fn insert_overlay_as(
1168:     path: &str,
1169:     kind: NodeKind,
1170:     mode: u16,
1171:     uid: u32,
1172:     gid: u32,
1173: ) -> Result<(), FsError> {
1174:     if path.len() > 256 {
1175:         return Err(FsError::InvalidPath);
1176:     }
1177:     let fs = unsafe { &mut *MOUNTED_RAMFS.0.get() };
1178:     let slot = fs
1179:         .overlay
1180:         .iter()
1181:         .position(|node| node.kind != NodeKind::Empty && node.path() == path)
1182:         .or_else(|| {
1183:             fs.overlay
1184:                 .iter()
1185:                 .position(|node| node.kind == NodeKind::Empty)
1186:         })
1187:         .ok_or(FsError::NoSpace)?;
1188:     let node = &mut fs.overlay[slot];
1189:     node.path.fill(0);
1190:     node.path[..path.len()].copy_from_slice(path.as_bytes());
1191:     node.path_length = path.len();
1192:     node.kind = kind;
1193:     node.data_start = 0;
1194:     node.data_capacity = 0;
1195:     node.mode = mode;
1196:     node.uid = uid;
1197:     node.gid = gid;
1198:     node.data_length = 0;
1199:     Ok(())
1200: }
1201: 
1202: fn join_child(parent: &str, name: &[u8], output: &mut [u8]) -> Option<usize> {
1203:     let prefix = parent.len() + usize::from(!parent.is_empty());
1204:     if prefix + name.len() > output.len() {
1205:         return None;
1206:     }
1207:     output[..parent.len()].copy_from_slice(parent.as_bytes());
1208:     if !parent.is_empty() {
1209:         output[parent.len()] = b'/';
1210:     }
1211:     output[prefix..prefix + name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
1212:     Some(prefix + name.len())
1213: }
1214: 
1215: fn immediate_child<'a>(path: &'a str, parent: &str) -> Option<Option<&'a [u8]>> {
1216:     let relative = if parent.is_empty() {
1217:         path
1218:     } else if path == parent {
1219:         return Some(None);
1220:     } else {
1221:         path.strip_prefix(parent)?.strip_prefix('/')?
1222:     };
1223:     if relative.is_empty() {
1224:         return Some(None);
1225:     }
1226:     Some(Some(relative.split('/').next()?.as_bytes()))
1227: }
1228: 
1229: fn append_name(output: &mut [u8], written: &mut usize, name: &[u8]) -> Option<()> {
1230:     if output[..*written]
1231:         .split(|byte| *byte == b'\n')
1232:         .any(|existing| existing == name)
1233:     {
1234:         return Some(());
1235:     }
1236:     let end = written.checked_add(name.len())?.checked_add(1)?;
1237:     if end > output.len() {
1238:         return None;
1239:     }
1240:     output[*written..*written + name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
1241:     output[end - 1] = b'\n';
1242:     *written = end;
1243:     Some(())
1244: }
1245: 
1246: fn field(bytes: &[u8]) -> &[u8] {
1247:     let end = bytes
1248:         .iter()
1249:         .position(|byte| *byte == 0)
1250:         .unwrap_or(bytes.len());
1251:     &bytes[..end]
1252: }
1253: 
1254: fn parse_octal(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<usize> {
1255:     let mut value = 0usize;
1256:     let mut found_digit = false;
1257:     for byte in bytes
1258:         .iter()
1259:         .copied()
1260:         .skip_while(|byte| *byte == b' ' || *byte == 0)
1261:     {
1262:         if byte == 0 || byte == b' ' {
1263:             break;
1264:         }
1265:         if !(b'0'..=b'7').contains(&byte) {
1266:             return None;
1267:         }
1268:         found_digit = true;
1269:         value = value.checked_mul(8)?.checked_add((byte - b'0') as usize)?;
1270:     }
1271:     found_digit.then_some(value)
1272: }
1273: 
1274: #[cfg(test)]
1275: mod tests {
1276:     use super::Archive;
1277: 
1278:     fn archive_with_file(prefix: &[u8], name: &[u8], contents: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
1279:         let size = (contents.len() + 511) & !511;
1280:         let mut bytes = vec![0u8; 512 + size + 1024];
1281:         bytes[..name.len()].copy_from_slice(name);
1282:         bytes[124..135].copy_from_slice(format!("{:011o}", contents.len()).as_bytes());
1283:         bytes[135] = 0;
1284:         bytes[156] = b'0';
1285:         bytes[257..262].copy_from_slice(b"ustar");
1286:         bytes[345..345 + prefix.len()].copy_from_slice(prefix);
1287:         bytes[512..512 + contents.len()].copy_from_slice(contents);
1288:         bytes
1289:     }
1290: 
1291:     #[test]
1292:     fn finds_root_file_and_accepts_leading_slash() {
1293:         let bytes = archive_with_file(b"", b"init", b"abc");
1294:         let archive = Archive::new(&bytes);
1295:         assert_eq!(archive.find("init"), Some(&b"abc"[..]));
1296:         assert_eq!(archive.find("/init"), Some(&b"abc"[..]));
1297:     }
1298: 
1299:     #[test]
1300:     fn rejects_missing_and_truncated_entries() {
1301:         let mut bytes = archive_with_file(b"", b"init", b"abc");

6: const DATA: u16 = 0x1f0;
7: const SECTOR_COUNT: u16 = 0x1f2;
8: const LBA_LOW: u16 = 0x1f3;
9: const LBA_MID: u16 = 0x1f4;
10: const LBA_HIGH: u16 = 0x1f5;
11: const DRIVE: u16 = 0x1f6;
12: const STATUS_COMMAND: u16 = 0x1f7;
13: const ALT_STATUS_CONTROL: u16 = 0x3f6;
14: const POLL_LIMIT: usize = 1_000_000;
15: const LBA28_SECTOR_LIMIT: u64 = 1 << 28;
16: 
17: struct DiskState {
18:     sectors: u64,
19:     ready: bool,
20: }
21: 
22: impl DiskState {
23:     const fn new() -> Self {
24:         Self {
25:             sectors: 0,
26:             ready: false,
27:         }
28:     }
29: }
30: 
31: struct SharedDisk(UnsafeCell<DiskState>);
32: unsafe impl Sync for SharedDisk {}
33: static DISK: SharedDisk = SharedDisk(UnsafeCell::new(DiskState::new()));
34: 
35: pub fn init() -> Result<u64, BlockError> {
36:     unsafe {
37:         *DISK.0.get() = DiskState::new();
38:     }
39:     identify()?;
40:     let mut identify_data = [0u8; SECTOR_SIZE];
41:     for word in 0..256 {
42:         let value = read_word(DATA);
43:         identify_data[word * 2..word * 2 + 2].copy_from_slice(&value.to_le_bytes());
44:     }
45: 
46:     let sectors = u32::from_le_bytes([
47:         identify_data[120],
48:         identify_data[121],
49:         identify_data[122],
50:         identify_data[123],
51:     ]) as u64;
52:     if sectors == 0 {
53:         return Err(BlockError::DeviceError);
54:     }
55: 
56:     unsafe {
57:         *DISK.0.get() = DiskState {
58:             sectors: sectors.min(LBA28_SECTOR_LIMIT),
59:             ready: true,
60:         };
61:     }
62:     Ok(sectors.min(LBA28_SECTOR_LIMIT))
63: }
64: 
65: fn identify() -> Result<(), BlockError> {
66:     write_port(DRIVE, 0xa0);
67:     io_delay();
68:     write_port(SECTOR_COUNT, 0);
69:     write_port(LBA_LOW, 0);
70:     write_port(LBA_MID, 0);
71:     write_port(LBA_HIGH, 0);
72:     write_port(STATUS_COMMAND, 0xec);
73:     io_delay();
74: 
75:     let status = read_port(STATUS_COMMAND);
76:     if status == 0 || status == 0xff {
77:         return Err(BlockError::NotReady);
78:     }
79: 
80:     for _ in 0..POLL_LIMIT {
81:         let status = read_port(STATUS_COMMAND);
82:         if status & 0x80 != 0 {
83:             core::hint::spin_loop();
84:         } else if status & 0x01 != 0 {
85:             return Err(BlockError::DeviceError);
86:         } else if status & 0x08 != 0 {
87:             return Ok(());
88:         } else {
89:             core::hint::spin_loop();
90:         }
91:     }
92:     Err(BlockError::Timeout)
93: }
94: 
95: fn read_sector(lba: u64, buffer: &mut [u8; SECTOR_SIZE]) -> Result<(), BlockError> {
96:     check_lba(lba)?;
97:     select_lba(lba);
98:     write_port(SECTOR_COUNT, 1);
99:     write_port(STATUS_COMMAND, 0x20);
100:     io_delay();
101:     wait_for_data()?;
102:     for chunk in buffer.chunks_exact_mut(2) {
103:         chunk.copy_from_slice(&read_word(DATA).to_le_bytes());
104:     }
105:     wait_not_busy()?;
106:     Ok(())
107: }
108: 
109: fn write_sector(lba: u64, buffer: &[u8; SECTOR_SIZE]) -> Result<(), BlockError> {
110:     check_lba(lba)?;
111:     select_lba(lba);
112:     write_port(SECTOR_COUNT, 1);
113:     write_port(STATUS_COMMAND, 0x30);
114:     io_delay();
115:     wait_for_data()?;
116:     for chunk in buffer.chunks_exact(2) {
117:         write_word(DATA, u16::from_le_bytes([chunk[0], chunk[1]]));
118:     }
119:     wait_not_busy()?;
120:     Ok(())
121: }
122: 
123: fn flush() -> Result<(), BlockError> {
124:     write_port(STATUS_COMMAND, 0xe7);
125:     io_delay();
126:     wait_not_busy()?;
127:     Ok(())
128: }
129: 
130: fn check_lba(lba: u64) -> Result<(), BlockError> {
131:     let state = unsafe { &*DISK.0.get() };
132:     if !state.ready {
133:         return Err(BlockError::NotReady);
134:     }
135:     if lba >= state.sectors || lba >= LBA28_SECTOR_LIMIT {
136:         return Err(BlockError::OutOfBounds);
137:     }
138:     Ok(())
139: }
140: 
141: fn select_lba(lba: u64) {
142:     write_port(DRIVE, 0xe0 | ((lba >> 24) as u8 & 0x0f));
143:     io_delay();
144:     write_port(LBA_LOW, lba as u8);
145:     write_port(LBA_MID, (lba >> 8) as u8);
146:     write_port(LBA_HIGH, (lba >> 16) as u8);
147: }
148: 
149: fn wait_for_data() -> Result<(), BlockError> {
150:     for _ in 0..POLL_LIMIT {
151:         let status = read_port(STATUS_COMMAND);
152:         if status & 0x01 != 0 || status & 0x20 != 0 {
153:             return Err(BlockError::DeviceError);
154:         }
155:         if status & 0x80 == 0 && status & 0x08 != 0 {
156:             return Ok(());
157:         }
158:         core::hint::spin_loop();
159:     }
160:     Err(BlockError::Timeout)
161: }
162: 
163: fn wait_not_busy() -> Result<(), BlockError> {
164:     for _ in 0..POLL_LIMIT {
165:         let status = read_port(STATUS_COMMAND);
166:         if status & 0x01 != 0 || status & 0x20 != 0 {
167:             return Err(BlockError::DeviceError);
168:         }
169:         if status & 0x80 == 0 {
170:             return Ok(());
171:         }
172:         core::hint::spin_loop();
173:     }
174:     Err(BlockError::Timeout)
175: }
176: 
177: fn io_delay() {
178:     for _ in 0..4 {
179:         let _ = read_port(ALT_STATUS_CONTROL);
180:     }
181: }
182: 
183: fn read_port(port: u16) -> u8 {
184:     let value: u8;
185:     unsafe {
186:         asm!(
187:             "in al, dx",
188:             in("dx") port,
189:             out("al") value,
190:             options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags)
191:         );
192:     }
193:     value
194: }
195:

197:     unsafe {
198:         asm!(
199:             "out dx, al",
200:             in("dx") port,
201:             in("al") value,
202:             options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags)
203:         );
204:     }
205: }
206:

213:             out("ax") value,

216:     }
217:     value
218: }
219:

224:             in("dx") port,
225:             in("ax") value,
226:             options(nomem, nostack, preserves_flags)

228:     }
229: }

1: //! Cópia entre o espaço de endereço do processo atual e o kernel.
2: //!
3: //! O mapa de páginas é consultado página a página (`AddressSpace::translate`),
4: //! então uma cópia que atravessa regiões não mapeadas falha sem tocar memória
5: //! inválida. Também concentra os helpers usados pelo loader (`zero_user_range`
6: //! e `copy_to_user` recebem o espaço explicitamente, porque o do processo novo
7: //! ainda não está ativo).
8: 
9: use core::ptr;
10: 
11: use crate::paging::AddressSpace;
12: 
13: use super::loader::LoadError;
14: use super::{PAGE_SIZE, SCHEDULER};
15: 
16: /// Executa `operation` com o espaço de endereço do processo atual.
17: pub fn with_current<T>(operation: impl FnOnce(AddressSpace) -> T) -> T {
18:     let scheduler = unsafe { &*SCHEDULER.0.get() };
19:     operation(
20:         scheduler.processes[scheduler.current]
21:             .address_space
22:             .expect("process address space"),
23:     )
24: }
25: 
26: /// Converte uma cópia explícita para o erro do loader.
27: pub fn copy_to_space_result(
28:     space: &AddressSpace,
29:     address: u64,
30:     input: &[u8],
31: ) -> Result<(), LoadError> {
32:     if copy_to_space(space, address, input) {
33:         Ok(())
34:     } else {
35:         Err(LoadError::InvalidMapping)
36:     }
37: }
38: 
39: /// Copia `output.len()` bytes do usuário para o kernel.
40: pub fn copy_from_current(address: u64, output: &mut [u8]) -> bool {
41:     with_current(|space| copy_from_space(&space, address, output))
42: }
43: 
44: /// Copia `input` do kernel para o usuário.
45: pub fn copy_to_current(address: u64, input: &[u8]) -> bool {
46:     with_current(|space| copy_to_space(&space, address, input))
47: }
48: 
49: /// Versão explícita do `copy_from_current` (espaço do processo novo).
50: pub fn copy_from_space(space: &AddressSpace, address: u64, output: &mut [u8]) -> bool {
51:     let mut copied = 0usize;
52:     while copied < output.len() {
53:         let Some(current) = address.checked_add(copied as u64) else {
54:             return false;
55:         };
56:         let Some(source) = space.translate(current) else {
57:             crate::serial_write(b"copy_from_space: translate failed at 0x");
58:             crate::serial_write_hex(current);
59:             crate::serial_write(b"\r\n");
60:             return false;
61:         };
62:         let available =
63:             ((PAGE_SIZE - (current & (PAGE_SIZE - 1))) as usize).min(output.len() - copied);
64:         unsafe {
65:             ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(source, output.as_mut_ptr().add(copied), available);
66:         }
67:         copied += available;
68:     }
69:     true
70: }
71: 
72: /// Versão explícita do `copy_to_current` (espaço do processo novo).
73: pub fn copy_to_space(space: &AddressSpace, address: u64, input: &[u8]) -> bool {
74:     let mut copied = 0usize;
75:     while copied < input.len() {
76:         let Some(current) = address.checked_add(copied as u64) else {
77:             return false;
78:         };
79:         let Some(destination) = space.translate(current) else {
80:             return false;
81:         };
82:         let available =
83:             ((PAGE_SIZE - (current & (PAGE_SIZE - 1))) as usize).min(input.len() - copied);
84:         unsafe {
85:             ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(input.as_ptr().add(copied), destination, available);
86:         }
87:         copied += available;
88:     }
89:     true
90: }

1: //! Minimal virtual filesystem layer.
2: //!
3: //! Before this module the routing between the persistent DFS root and the
4: //! volatile USTAR/RAMFS root was open-coded as `if crate::dfs::is_mounted()` at
5: //! the top of nearly every function in `ramfs.rs`. That coupling made the two
6: //! backends impossible to reason about independently.
7: //!
8: //! [`FileSystem`] is the (deliberately small) contract every backend implements,
9: //! and [`Vfs`] is the single place that decides which backend is active for a
10: //! given call. The rest of the kernel keeps using the `ramfs::*` facade, which
11: //! now delegates here instead of branching on the mounted state itself.
12: //!
13: //! This is intentionally *not* a complete VFS: there is no inode/dentry cache,
14: //! no mount table, no `switch_root`/`pivot_root`, and no per-file `File` object.
15: //! It removes the duplicated routing and gives a seam to grow those later.
16: 
17: use core::cell::UnsafeCell;
18: 
19: use crate::dfs::{self, DfsError, Metadata};
20: use crate::ramfs::{self, FsError};
21: 
22: /// Error type returned by every [`FileSystem`] operation.
23: ///
24: /// It unifies the two backend error enums so callers do not need to know which
25: /// backend served the request. Codes mirror the syscall ABI negative errnos via
26: /// [`VfsError::to_errno`].
27: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
28: pub enum VfsError {
29:     InvalidPath,
30:     NotFound,
31:     NotDirectory,
32:     IsDirectory,
33:     AlreadyExists,
34:     DirectoryNotEmpty,
35:     NoSpace,
36:     Io,
37: }
38: 
39: impl VfsError {
40:     /// Maps to the negative errno used by the syscall ABI.
41:     pub fn to_errno(self) -> i64 {
42:         match self {
43:             VfsError::InvalidPath => -22,        // EINVAL
44:             VfsError::NotFound => -2,            // ENOENT
45:             VfsError::NotDirectory => -20,       // ENOTDIR
46:             VfsError::IsDirectory => -21,        // EISDIR
47:             VfsError::AlreadyExists => -17,      // EEXIST
48:             VfsError::DirectoryNotEmpty => -39,  // ENOTEMPTY
49:             VfsError::NoSpace => -28,            // ENOSPC
50:             VfsError::Io => -5,                  // EIO
51:         }
52:     }
53: }
54: 
55: impl From<FsError> for VfsError {
56:     fn from(error: FsError) -> Self {
57:         match error {
58:             FsError::InvalidPath => VfsError::InvalidPath,
59:             FsError::NotFound => VfsError::NotFound,
60:             FsError::NotDirectory => VfsError::NotDirectory,
61:             FsError::IsDirectory => VfsError::IsDirectory,
62:             FsError::AlreadyExists => VfsError::AlreadyExists,
63:             FsError::DirectoryNotEmpty => VfsError::DirectoryNotEmpty,
64:             FsError::NoSpace => VfsError::NoSpace,
65:         }
66:     }
67: }
68: 
69: impl From<DfsError> for VfsError {
70:     fn from(error: DfsError) -> Self {
71:         match error {
72:             DfsError::InvalidPath => VfsError::InvalidPath,
73:             DfsError::NotFound => VfsError::NotFound,
74:             DfsError::NotDirectory => VfsError::NotDirectory,
75:             DfsError::IsDirectory => VfsError::IsDirectory,
76:             DfsError::AlreadyExists => VfsError::AlreadyExists,
77:             DfsError::DirectoryNotEmpty => VfsError::DirectoryNotEmpty,
78:             DfsError::NoSpace | DfsError::JournalFull => VfsError::NoSpace,
79:             DfsError::Block(_) | DfsError::InvalidFilesystem | DfsError::CorruptMetadata => {
80:                 VfsError::Io
81:             }
82:         }
83:     }
84: }
85: 
86: /// The operations a mounted filesystem must provide.
87: ///
88: /// Implementations are stateless handles: the concrete backend (USTAR archive,
89: /// DFS on disk) holds its own state and this trait only describes the verbs.
90: pub trait FileSystem {
91:     /// Reads a whole file whose size was previously bounded by the caller.
92:     fn read_static(&self, path: &str) -> Option<&'static [u8]>;
93: 
94:     /// Reads up to `output.len()` bytes starting at `offset`.
95:     fn read_at(&self, path: &str, offset: usize, output: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, VfsError>;
96: 
97:     fn file_len(&self, path: &str) -> Result<usize, VfsError>;
98: 
99:     fn metadata(&self, path: &str) -> Option<Metadata>;
100:
